===== api/app/Http/Controllers/Concerns/ResolvesFacility.php ===== (45 lignes, 1 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers\Concerns;

use App\Models\Facility;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

/**
 * Trait à inclure dans chaque contrôleur métier.
 * Fournit facility() et un helper pour les réponses JSON d'erreur uniformes.
 */
trait ResolvesFacility
{
    protected function facility(Request $request): Facility
    {
        $facility = $request->attributes->get('facility');

        if (! $facility instanceof Facility) {
            // Fallback : si le middleware n'est pas monté, on résout manuellement
            $facility = $request->user()?->currentFacility;

            if (! $facility) {
                abort(409, 'Aucun établissement actif. Choisissez-en un dans Établissements.');
            }

            if (! $facility->is_active) {
                abort(403, 'Votre établissement actif est désactivé.');
            }
        }

        // PHPStan : currentFacility() retourne Model, on assert le type réel
        assert($facility instanceof Facility);

        return $facility;
    }

    protected function jsonError(int $status, string $code, string $message): JsonResponse
    {
        return response()->json([
            'error' => ['code' => $code, 'message' => $message],
        ], $status);
    }
}


===== api/app/Http/Controllers/AppointmentController.php ===== (219 lignes, 7 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Http\Controllers\Concerns\ResolvesFacility;
use App\Http\Requests\Appointment\StoreAppointmentRequest;
use App\Models\Appointment;
use App\Models\Facility;
use App\Models\Patient;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;

class AppointmentController extends Controller
{
    use ResolvesFacility;

    public function index(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $query = Appointment::inFacility($facility)
            ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name', 'practitioner:id,name'])
            ->orderBy('scheduled_at');

        $date = $request->query('date');

        if ($date) {
            $query->whereDate('scheduled_at', $date);
        }

        return response()->json([
            'appointments' => $query->get()
                ->map(fn ($a) => $this->payload($a)),
        ]);
    }

    public function practitioners(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $users = $facility->users()
            ->orderBy('name')
            ->get(['id', 'name']);

        return response()->json([
            'practitioners' => $users->map(fn ($u) => [
                'id' => $u->id,
                'name' => $u->name,
            ]),
        ]);
    }

    public function store(StoreAppointmentRequest $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);
        $data = $request->validated();

        $patient = Patient::inFacility($facility)->find($data['patient_id']);

        if (! $patient) {
            abort(422, 'Ce patient n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
        }

        $practitioner = User::find($data['practitioner_id']);

        if (! $practitioner->isMemberOf($facility)) {
            abort(422, 'Ce praticien n\'appartient pas à votre établissement.');
        }

        $start = Carbon::parse($data['scheduled_at']);
        $duration = $data['duration_minutes'] ?? 30;

        if ($this->hasConflict($facility, $practitioner->id, $start, $duration)) {
            abort(422, 'Conflit d\'agenda : le praticien a déjà un rendez-vous sur ce créneau.');
        }

        $appointment = Appointment::create([
            'facility_id' => $facility->id,
            'patient_id' => $patient->id,
            'practitioner_id' => $practitioner->id,
            'created_by' => $request->user()->id,
            'scheduled_at' => $start,
            'duration_minutes' => $duration,
            'status' => 'scheduled',
            'reason' => $data['reason'] ?? null,
        ]);

        return response()->json(
            ['appointment' => $this->payload($appointment->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'practitioner:id,name'
            ))],
            201
        );
    }

    public function show(Request $request, int $appointmentId): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $appointment = Appointment::inFacility($facility)
            ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name', 'practitioner:id,name'])
            ->findOrFail($appointmentId);

        return response()->json([
            'appointment' => $this->payload($appointment),
        ]);
    }

    public function update(Request $request, int $appointmentId): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);
        $appointment = Appointment::inFacility($facility)->findOrFail($appointmentId);

        $data = $request->validate([
            'status' => ['sometimes', 'string', 'in:'.implode(',', Appointment::STATUSES)],
            'scheduled_at' => ['sometimes', 'date', 'after:now'],
            'duration_minutes' => ['sometimes', 'integer', 'min:5', 'max:480'],
            'reason' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:500'],
            'notes' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'string'],
        ], [
            'status.in' => 'Statut invalide.',
        ]);

        if (isset($data['scheduled_at'])) {
            $start = Carbon::parse($data['scheduled_at']);
            $duration = $data['duration_minutes'] ?? $appointment->duration_minutes;

            if ($appointment->status !== 'cancelled') {
                if ($this->hasConflict($facility, $appointment->practitioner_id, $start, $duration, $appointment->id)) {
                    abort(422, 'Conflit d\'agenda : le praticien a déjà un rendez-vous sur ce créneau.');
                }
            }

            $appointment->scheduled_at = $start;
        }

        if (isset($data['duration_minutes'])) {
            $appointment->duration_minutes = $data['duration_minutes'];
        }

        if (array_key_exists('reason', $data)) {
            $appointment->reason = $data['reason'];
        }

        if (array_key_exists('notes', $data)) {
            $appointment->notes = $data['notes'];
        }

        if (isset($data['status'])) {
            $appointment->status = $data['status'];
        }

        $appointment->save();

        return response()->json([
            'appointment' => $this->payload($appointment->fresh()->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'practitioner:id,name'
            )),
        ]);
    }

    private function hasConflict(
        Facility $facility,
        int $practitionerId,
        $start,
        int $durationMinutes,
        ?int $ignoreId = null
    ): bool {
        $end = (clone $start)->addMinutes($durationMinutes);
        $maxDuration = 480;

        $query = Appointment::inFacility($facility)
            ->where('practitioner_id', $practitionerId)
            ->whereIn('status', ['scheduled', 'confirmed'])
            ->where('scheduled_at', '<', $end)
            ->where('scheduled_at', '>=', (clone $start)->subMinutes($maxDuration));

        if ($ignoreId !== null) {
            $query->where('id', '!=', $ignoreId);
        }

        foreach ($query->get(['scheduled_at', 'duration_minutes']) as $existing) {
            $existingEnd = $existing->scheduled_at->addMinutes($existing->duration_minutes);

            if ($existingEnd > $start) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private function payload(Appointment $a): array
    {
        return [
            'id' => $a->id,
            'patient' => [
                'id' => $a->patient->id,
                'local_code' => $a->patient->local_code,
                'first_name' => $a->patient->first_name,
                'last_name' => $a->patient->last_name,
            ],
            'practitioner' => [
                'id' => $a->practitioner->id,
                'name' => $a->practitioner->name,
            ],
            'scheduled_at' => $a->scheduled_at->format('Y-m-d H:i'),
            'duration_minutes' => $a->duration_minutes,
            'status' => $a->status,
            'reason' => $a->reason,
            'notes' => $a->notes,
        ];
    }
}


===== api/app/Http/Controllers/AuditLogController.php ===== (91 lignes, 3 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Http\Controllers\Concerns\ResolvesFacility;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Spatie\Activitylog\Models\Activity;

class AuditLogController extends Controller
{
    use ResolvesFacility;

    /**
     * Liste les logs d'audit de l'établissement courant.
     *
     * Query params :
     *   subject_type  (ex: Patient, Invoice)  - optionnel
     *   subject_id    (int)                   - optionnel
     *   event         (created, updated, deleted) - optionnel
     *   from          (Y-m-d)                 - optionnel
     *   to            (Y-m-d)                 - optionnel
     *   per_page      (int, 1-100, défaut 25)
     */
    public function index(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        if (! $request->user()->can('reports.view')) {
            abort(403, 'Vous n\'avez pas accès aux logs d\'audit.');
        }

        $query = Activity::query()
            ->where('properties->facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->with(['causer:id,name,email'])
            ->orderByDesc('created_at');

        if ($type = $request->query('subject_type')) {
            $query->where('subject_type', 'App\\Models\\'.$type);
        }

        if ($id = $request->query('subject_id')) {
            $query->where('subject_id', (int) $id);
        }

        if ($event = $request->query('event')) {
            $query->where('event', $event);
        }

        if ($from = $request->query('from')) {
            $query->where('created_at', '>=', $from.' 00:00:00');
        }

        if ($to = $request->query('to')) {
            $query->where('created_at', '<=', $to.' 23:59:59');
        }

        $perPage = min((int) $request->query('per_page', 25), 100);
        $logs = $query->paginate($perPage);

        return response()->json([
            'activity_logs' => $logs->getCollection()->map(fn ($activity) => [
                'id' => $activity->id,
                'log_name' => $activity->log_name,
                'event' => $activity->event,
                'description' => $activity->description,
                'subject' => [
                    'type' => $activity->subject_type,
                    'id' => $activity->subject_id,
                ],
                'causer' => $activity->causer ? [
                    'id' => $activity->causer->id,
                    'name' => $activity->causer->name,
                    'email' => $activity->causer->email,
                ] : null,
                'changes' => [
                    'old' => $activity->properties['old'] ?? null,
                    'new' => $activity->properties['attributes'] ?? null,
                ],
                'created_at' => $activity->created_at->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
            ]),
            'pagination' => [
                'current_page' => $logs->currentPage(),
                'per_page' => $logs->perPage(),
                'total' => $logs->total(),
                'last_page' => $logs->lastPage(),
            ],
        ]);
    }
}


===== api/app/Http/Controllers/AuthController.php ===== (70 lignes, 2 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Http\Requests\Auth\LoginRequest;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Hash;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;

class AuthController extends Controller
{
    public function login(LoginRequest $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $data = $request->validated();

        $user = User::where('email', $data['email'])->first();

        if (! $user || ! Hash::check($data['password'], $user->password)) {
            throw ValidationException::withMessages([
                'email' => 'Identifiants incorrects.',
            ]);
        }

        if (! $user->is_active) {
            abort(403, 'Compte désactivé. Contactez un administrateur.');
        }

        $user->tokens()->where('name', 'spa')->delete();

        return response()->json([
            'token' => $user->createToken('spa')->plainTextToken,
            'user' => $this->payload($user),
        ]);
    }

    public function me(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        return response()->json([
            'user' => $this->payload($request->user()),
        ]);
    }

    public function logout(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $token = $request->user()->currentAccessToken();

        if ($token && method_exists($token, 'delete')) {
            $token->delete();
        }

        return response()->json(['message' => 'Déconnecté.']);
    }

    private function payload(User $user): array
    {
        $user->loadMissing('roles.permissions');

        return [
            'id' => $user->id,
            'name' => $user->name,
            'email' => $user->email,
            'phone' => $user->phone,
            'roles' => $user->getRoleNames(),
            'permissions' => $user->getAllPermissions()->pluck('name'),
        ];
    }
}


===== api/app/Http/Controllers/ConsultationController.php ===== (163 lignes, 6 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Http\Controllers\Concerns\ResolvesFacility;
use App\Models\Appointment;
use App\Models\Consultation;
use App\Models\Patient;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class ConsultationController extends Controller
{
    use ResolvesFacility;

    public function index(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $query = Consultation::inFacility($facility)
            ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name', 'practitioner:id,name'])
            ->orderByDesc('created_at');

        $patientId = $request->query('patient_id');

        if ($patientId !== null) {
            $query->where('patient_id', (int) $patientId);
        }

        return response()->json([
            'consultations' => $query->get()
                ->map(fn ($c) => $this->payload($c)),
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $data = $request->validate([
            'patient_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:patients,id'],
            'appointment_id' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'exists:appointments,id'],
            'chief_complaint' => ['required', 'string', 'max:1000'],
            'examination' => ['nullable', 'string'],
            'diagnosis' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:1000'],
            'treatment' => ['nullable', 'string'],
            'follow_up_date' => ['nullable', 'date'],
        ], [
            'chief_complaint.required' => 'Le motif de consultation est obligatoire.',
            'patient_id.exists' => 'Patient inconnu.',
            'appointment_id.exists' => 'Rendez-vous inconnu.',
        ]);

        $patient = Patient::inFacility($facility)->find($data['patient_id']);

        if (! $patient) {
            abort(422, 'Ce patient n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
        }

        $appointment = null;

        if (! empty($data['appointment_id'])) {
            $appointment = Appointment::inFacility($facility)->find($data['appointment_id']);

            if (! $appointment) {
                abort(422, 'Ce rendez-vous n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
            }
        }

        $consultation = Consultation::create([
            'facility_id' => $facility->id,
            'patient_id' => $patient->id,
            'appointment_id' => $appointment?->id,
            'practitioner_id' => $request->user()->id,
            'created_by' => $request->user()->id,
            'chief_complaint' => $data['chief_complaint'],
            'examination' => $data['examination'] ?? null,
            'diagnosis' => $data['diagnosis'] ?? null,
            'treatment' => $data['treatment'] ?? null,
            'follow_up_date' => $data['follow_up_date'] ?? null,
        ]);

        if ($appointment && in_array($appointment->status, ['scheduled', 'confirmed'], true)) {
            $appointment->status = 'done';
            $appointment->save();
        }

        return response()->json(
            ['consultation' => $this->payload($consultation->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'practitioner:id,name'
            ))],
            201
        );
    }

    public function show(Request $request, int $consultationId): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $consultation = Consultation::inFacility($facility)
            ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name', 'practitioner:id,name'])
            ->findOrFail($consultationId);

        return response()->json([
            'consultation' => $this->payload($consultation),
        ]);
    }

    public function update(Request $request, int $consultationId): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);
        $consultation = Consultation::inFacility($facility)->findOrFail($consultationId);

        $data = $request->validate([
            'chief_complaint' => ['sometimes', 'string', 'max:1000'],
            'examination' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'string'],
            'diagnosis' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'string', 'max:1000'],
            'treatment' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'string'],
            'follow_up_date' => ['sometimes', 'nullable', 'date'],
        ]);

        foreach (['chief_complaint', 'examination', 'diagnosis', 'treatment', 'follow_up_date'] as $field) {
            if (array_key_exists($field, $data)) {
                $consultation->{$field} = $data[$field];
            }
        }

        $consultation->save();

        return response()->json([
            'consultation' => $this->payload($consultation->fresh()->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'practitioner:id,name'
            )),
        ]);
    }

    private function payload(Consultation $c): array
    {
        return [
            'id' => $c->id,
            'patient' => [
                'id' => $c->patient->id,
                'local_code' => $c->patient->local_code,
                'first_name' => $c->patient->first_name,
                'last_name' => $c->patient->last_name,
            ],
            'practitioner' => [
                'id' => $c->practitioner->id,
                'name' => $c->practitioner->name,
            ],
            'appointment_id' => $c->appointment_id,
            'chief_complaint' => $c->chief_complaint,
            'examination' => $c->examination,
            'diagnosis' => $c->diagnosis,
            'treatment' => $c->treatment,
            'follow_up_date' => $c->follow_up_date?->format('Y-m-d'),
            'created_at' => $c->created_at->format('Y-m-d H:i'),
        ];
    }
}

===== api/app/Http/Controllers/FacilityController.php ===== (117 lignes, 4 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Models\Facility;
use App\Models\User;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class FacilityController extends Controller
{
    public function index(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        $facilities = $user->can('facilities.manage')
                    ? Facility::query()->orderBy('name')->get()
                    : $user->facilities()->orderBy('name')->get();

        return response()->json([
            'facilities' => $facilities,
            'current_facility_id' => $user->current_facility_id,
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

        $data = $request->validate([
            'code' => ['required', 'string', 'max:20', 'unique:facilities,code'],
            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'city' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'address' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'phone' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:30'],
            'email' => ['nullable', 'email'],
        ], [
            'code.required' => 'Le code établissement est obligatoire.',
            'code.unique' => 'Ce code établissement existe déjà.',
            'name.required' => 'Le nom de l\'établissement est obligatoire.',
        ]);

        $facility = Facility::create($data);
        $request->user()->facilities()->syncWithoutDetaching([$facility->id]);

        return response()->json(['facility' => $facility], 201);
    }

    public function update(Request $request, Facility $facility): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

        $data = $request->validate([
            'name' => ['sometimes', 'required', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'city' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'address' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:255'],
            'phone' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:30'],
            'email' => ['nullable', 'email'],
            'is_active' => ['sometimes', 'boolean'],
        ]);

        $facility->update($data);

        return response()->json(['facility' => $facility->fresh()]);
    }

    public function switch(Request $request, Facility $facility): JsonResponse
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        if (! $user->isMemberOf($facility)) {
            abort(403, 'Vous n\'êtes pas membre de cet établissement.');
        }

        if (! $facility->is_active) {
            abort(403, 'Cet établissement est désactivé.');
        }

        $user->forceFill(['current_facility_id' => $facility->id])->save();

        return response()->json([
            'message' => 'Établissement actif mis à jour.',
            'current_facility_id' => $facility->id,
        ]);
    }

    public function attachUser(Request $request, Facility $facility): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->authorizeManage($request);

        $data = $request->validate([
            'email' => ['required', 'email'],
        ]);

        // VAGUE1: anti-énumération e-mail
        $target = User::where('email', $data['email'])->first();

        if (! $target) {
            abort(422, 'Impossible de rattacher cet utilisateur. Vérifiez l\'adresse e-mail.');
        }
        $facility->users()->syncWithoutDetaching([$target->id]);

        return response()->json([
            'message' => 'Utilisateur rattaché à l\'établissement.',
            'user_id' => $target->id,
            'facility_id' => $facility->id,
        ]);
    }

    private function authorizeManage(Request $request): void
    {
        if (! $request->user()->can('facilities.manage')) {
            abort(403, 'Action réservée aux administrateurs.');
        }
    }
}


===== api/app/Http/Controllers/InvoiceController.php ===== (253 lignes, 8 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Http\Controllers\Concerns\ResolvesFacility;
use App\Http\Requests\Invoice\PayInvoiceRequest;
use App\Http\Requests\Invoice\StoreInvoiceRequest;
use App\Models\Consultation;
use App\Models\Invoice;
use App\Models\InvoiceItem;
use App\Models\Patient;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class InvoiceController extends Controller
{
    use ResolvesFacility;

    public function index(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $query = Invoice::inFacility($facility)
            ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name'])
            ->withSum('payments as paid_amount', 'amount')
            ->orderByDesc('created_at');

        $patientId = $request->query('patient_id');

        if ($patientId !== null) {
            $query->where('patient_id', (int) $patientId);
        }

        $status = $request->query('status');

        if ($status) {
            $query->where('status', $status);
        }

        return response()->json([
            'invoices' => $query->get()
                ->map(fn ($i) => $this->payload($i)),
        ]);
    }

    public function store(StoreInvoiceRequest $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);
        $data = $request->validated();

        $patient = Patient::inFacility($facility)->find($data['patient_id']);

        if (! $patient) {
            abort(422, 'Ce patient n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
        }

        $consultation = null;

        if (! empty($data['consultation_id'])) {
            $consultation = Consultation::inFacility($facility)->find($data['consultation_id']);

            if (! $consultation) {
                abort(422, 'Cette consultation n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
            }
        }

        $invoice = DB::transaction(function () use ($facility, $patient, $consultation, $data, $request) {
            $total = 0;

            foreach ($data['items'] as $item) {
                $total += $item['quantity'] * $item['unit_price'];
            }

            $record = Invoice::create([
                'facility_id' => $facility->id,
                'patient_id' => $patient->id,
                'consultation_id' => $consultation?->id,
                'created_by' => $request->user()->id,
                'number' => 'TMP-'.uniqid(),
                'status' => 'draft',
                'total_amount' => $total,
                'due_date' => $data['due_date'] ?? null,
            ]);

            $record->number = 'FAC-'.str_pad((string) $record->id, 5, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);
            $record->save();

            foreach ($data['items'] as $item) {
                InvoiceItem::create([
                    'invoice_id' => $record->id,
                    'label' => $item['label'],
                    'quantity' => $item['quantity'],
                    'unit_price' => $item['unit_price'],
                    'amount' => $item['quantity'] * $item['unit_price'],
                ]);
            }

            return $record;
        });

        return response()->json(
            ['invoice' => $this->payload($invoice->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'items',
                'payments'
            ))],
            201
        );
    }

    public function show(Request $request, int $invoiceId): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $invoice = Invoice::inFacility($facility)
            ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name', 'items', 'payments'])
            ->withSum('payments as paid_amount', 'amount')
            ->findOrFail($invoiceId);

        return response()->json([
            'invoice' => $this->payload($invoice),
        ]);
    }

    public function update(Request $request, int $invoiceId): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);
        $invoice = Invoice::inFacility($facility)->findOrFail($invoiceId);

        $data = $request->validate([
            'status' => ['required', 'string', 'in:issued,cancelled'],
        ], [
            'status.in' => 'Transition invalide (émettre ou annuler uniquement).',
        ]);

        if ($invoice->status === 'paid') {
            abort(422, 'Cette facture est payée et ne peut plus être modifiée.');
        }

        if ($invoice->status === 'cancelled') {
            abort(422, 'Cette facture est annulée et ne peut plus être modifiée.');
        }

        if ($data['status'] === 'cancelled') {
            $paid = (int) $invoice->payments()->sum('amount');

            if ($paid > 0) {
                abort(422, 'Impossible d\'annuler une facture ayant encaissé '.$paid.' XOF.');
            }
        }

        if ($data['status'] === 'issued') {
            if ($invoice->status !== 'draft') {
                abort(422, 'Seule une facture brouillon peut être émise.');
            }
        }

        $invoice->status = $data['status'];
        $invoice->save();

        return response()->json([
            'invoice' => $this->payload($invoice->fresh()->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'items',
                'payments'
            )),
        ]);
    }

    public function pay(PayInvoiceRequest $request, int $invoiceId): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);
        $data = $request->validated();

        $invoice = DB::transaction(function () use ($facility, $invoiceId, $data, $request) {
            $record = Invoice::inFacility($facility)
                ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name', 'items', 'payments'])
                ->lockForUpdate()
                ->findOrFail($invoiceId);

            if ($record->status !== 'issued') {
                abort(422, 'Seule une facture émise peut être encaissée (statut actuel : '.$record->status.').');
            }

            $paid = (int) $record->payments()->sum('amount');
            $remaining = $record->total_amount - $paid;

            if ($data['amount'] > $remaining) {
                abort(422, 'Montant supérieur au reste à payer ('.$remaining.' XOF).');
            }

            $record->payments()->create([
                'amount' => $data['amount'],
                'method' => $data['method'],
                'received_by' => $request->user()->id,
                'paid_at' => now(),
            ]);

            $paid += $data['amount'];

            if ($paid >= $record->total_amount) {
                $record->status = 'paid';
                $record->save();
            }

            return $record->fresh()->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'items',
                'payments'
            );
        });

        return response()->json([
            'invoice' => $this->payload($invoice),
        ]);
    }

    private function payload(Invoice $i): array
    {
        $paidAmount = (int) ($i->paid_amount ?? $i->payments->sum('amount'));

        return [
            'id' => $i->id,
            'number' => $i->number,
            'patient' => [
                'id' => $i->patient->id,
                'local_code' => $i->patient->local_code,
                'first_name' => $i->patient->first_name,
                'last_name' => $i->patient->last_name,
            ],
            'consultation_id' => $i->consultation_id,
            'status' => $i->status,
            'total_amount' => (int) $i->total_amount,
            'paid_amount' => $paidAmount,
            'remaining_amount' => (int) $i->total_amount - $paidAmount,
            'due_date' => $i->due_date?->format('Y-m-d'),
            'items' => $i->items->map(fn ($item) => [
                'label' => $item->label,
                'quantity' => $item->quantity,
                'unit_price' => (int) $item->unit_price,
                'amount' => (int) $item->amount,
            ]),
            'payments' => $i->payments->map(fn ($p) => [
                'amount' => (int) $p->amount,
                'method' => $p->method,
                'paid_at' => $p->paid_at->format('Y-m-d H:i'),
            ]),
            'created_at' => $i->created_at->format('Y-m-d H:i'),
        ];
    }
}

<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Http\Controllers\Concerns\ResolvesFacility;
use App\Models\Consultation;
use App\Models\LabRequest;
use App\Models\Patient;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class LabRequestController extends Controller
{
    use ResolvesFacility;

    public function index(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $query = LabRequest::inFacility($facility)
            ->with([
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'requester:id,name',
                'result.resultedBy:id,name',
            ])
            ->orderByDesc('created_at');

        $patientId = $request->query('patient_id');

        if ($patientId !== null) {
            $query->where('patient_id', (int) $patientId);
        }

        $status = $request->query('status');

        if ($status) {
            $query->where('status', $status);
        }

        return response()->json([
            'lab_requests' => $query->get()
                ->map(fn ($r) => $this->payload($r)),
        ]);
    }

    public function store(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $data = $request->validate([
            'patient_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:patients,id'],
            'consultation_id' => ['nullable', 'integer', 'exists:consultations,id'],
            'panel' => ['required', 'string', 'max:200'],
            'priority' => ['nullable', 'string', 'in:routine,urgent'],
            'notes' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:500'],
        ], [
            'panel.required' => 'Le type d\'analyse est obligatoire.',
            'patient_id.exists' => 'Patient inconnu.',
            'consultation_id.exists' => 'Consultation inconnue.',
            'priority.in' => 'Priorité invalide.',
        ]);

        $patient = Patient::inFacility($facility)->find($data['patient_id']);

        if (! $patient) {
            abort(422, 'Ce patient n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
        }

        $consultation = null;

        if (! empty($data['consultation_id'])) {
            $consultation = Consultation::inFacility($facility)->find($data['consultation_id']);

            if (! $consultation) {
                abort(422, 'Cette consultation n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
            }
        }

        $labRequest = LabRequest::create([
            'facility_id' => $facility->id,
            'patient_id' => $patient->id,
            'consultation_id' => $consultation?->id,
            'requested_by' => $request->user()->id,
            'panel' => $data['panel'],
            'priority' => $data['priority'] ?? 'routine',
            'status' => 'pending',
            'notes' => $data['notes'] ?? null,
        ]);

        return response()->json(
            ['lab_request' => $this->payload($labRequest->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'requester:id,name'
            ))],
            201
        );
    }

    public function show(Request $request, int $labRequestId): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $labRequest = LabRequest::inFacility($facility)
            ->with([
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'requester:id,name',
                'result.resultedBy:id,name',
            ])
            ->findOrFail($labRequestId);

        return response()->json([
            'lab_request' => $this->payload($labRequest),
        ]);
    }

    public function update(Request $request, int $labRequestId): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);
        $labRequest = LabRequest::inFacility($facility)->findOrFail($labRequestId);

        if ($labRequest->status === 'completed') {
            abort(422, 'Cette demande est complétée et ne peut plus être modifiée.');
        }

        $data = $request->validate([
            'status' => ['required', 'string', 'in:'.implode(',', ['pending', 'in_progress', 'cancelled'])],
        ], [
            'status.in' => 'Statut invalide (la complétion passe par la saisie du résultat).',
        ]);

        $labRequest->status = $data['status'];
        $labRequest->save();

        return response()->json([
            'lab_request' => $this->payload($labRequest->fresh()->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'requester:id,name',
                'result.resultedBy:id,name'
            )),
        ]);
    }

    public function storeResult(Request $request, int $labRequestId): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);
        $labRequest = LabRequest::inFacility($facility)->findOrFail($labRequestId);

        if ($labRequest->status === 'cancelled') {
            abort(422, 'Cette demande d\'analyse a été annulée.');
        }

        if ($labRequest->status === 'completed') {
            abort(422, 'Cette demande a déjà un résultat enregistré.');
        }

        $data = $request->validate([
            'values' => ['required', 'string', 'max:4000'],
            'conclusion' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:1000'],
        ], [
            'values.required' => 'Les valeurs des analyses sont obligatoires.',
        ]);

        $labRequest->result()->updateOrCreate(
            [],
            [
                'values' => $data['values'],
                'conclusion' => $data['conclusion'] ?? null,
                'resulted_by' => $request->user()->id,
                'resulted_at' => now(),
            ]
        );

        $labRequest->status = 'completed';
        $labRequest->save();

        return response()->json([
            'lab_request' => $this->payload($labRequest->fresh()->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'requester:id,name',
                'result.resultedBy:id,name'
            )),
        ]);
    }

    private function payload(LabRequest $r): array
    {
        return [
            'id' => $r->id,
            'patient' => [
                'id' => $r->patient->id,
                'local_code' => $r->patient->local_code,
                'first_name' => $r->patient->first_name,
                'last_name' => $r->patient->last_name,
            ],
            'requested_by' => $r->requester?->name,
            'consultation_id' => $r->consultation_id,
            'panel' => $r->panel,
            'priority' => $r->priority,
            'status' => $r->status,
            'notes' => $r->notes,
            'result' => $r->result ? [
                'values' => $r->result->values,
                'conclusion' => $r->result->conclusion,
                'resulted_by' => $r->result->resultedBy?->name,
                'resulted_at' => $r->result->resulted_at?->format('Y-m-d H:i'),
            ] : null,
            'created_at' => $r->created_at->format('Y-m-d H:i'),
        ];
    }
}


===== api/app/Http/Controllers/PatientController.php ===== (182 lignes, 6 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Http\Controllers\Concerns\ResolvesFacility;
use App\Http\Requests\Patient\StorePatientRequest;
use App\Http\Requests\Patient\UpdatePatientRequest;
use App\Models\Facility;
use App\Models\MasterPatientIndex;
use App\Models\Patient;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

class PatientController extends Controller
{
    use ResolvesFacility;

    public function index(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $patients = $facility->patients()
            ->with('mpi:id,hospiflow_code')
            ->orderBy('last_name')
            ->orderBy('first_name')
            ->get();

        return response()->json([
            'facility' => ['id' => $facility->id, 'code' => $facility->code],
            'patients' => $patients->map(fn (Patient $p) => $this->payload($p)),
        ]);
    }

    public function search(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);
        $q = trim((string) $request->query('q', ''));

        if ($q === '') {
            return response()->json(['results' => []]);
        }

        $patients = $facility->patients()
            ->with('mpi:id,hospiflow_code')
            ->where(function ($w) use ($q) {
                $w->whereRaw($this->portableLike('first_name'), [$this->likePattern($q)])
                    ->orWhereRaw($this->portableLike('last_name'), [$this->likePattern($q)])
                    ->orWhereRaw($this->portableLike('local_code'), [$this->likePattern($q)])
                    ->orWhereRaw($this->portableLike('phone'), [$this->likePattern($q)])
                    ->orWhereHas('mpi', function ($m) use ($q) {
                        $m->whereRaw('LOWER(hospiflow_code) LIKE LOWER(?)', [$this->likePattern($q)]);
                    });
            })
            ->limit(50)
            ->get();

        return response()->json([
            'results' => $patients->map(fn (Patient $p) => $this->payload($p)),
        ]);
    }

    public function store(StorePatientRequest $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);
        $data = $request->validated();

        $mpi = null;

        if (! empty($data['mpi_code'])) {
            $mpi = MasterPatientIndex::where('hospiflow_code', $data['mpi_code'])->first();

            if (! $mpi) {
                abort(422, 'Identifiant réseau HOSPIFLOW inconnu.');
            }
        }

        if (! $mpi) {
            $mpi = MasterPatientIndex::create([
                'first_name' => $data['first_name'],
                'last_name' => $data['last_name'],
                'date_of_birth' => $data['date_of_birth'] ?? null,
                'gender' => $data['gender'] ?? null,
                'phone' => $data['phone'] ?? null,
            ]);
        }

        $existing = $facility->patients()->where('mpi_id', $mpi->id)->first();

        if ($existing) {
            return response()->json([
                'patient' => $this->payload($existing->load('mpi:id,hospiflow_code')),
                'message' => 'Ce patient réseau existe déjà dans cet établissement.',
            ]);
        }

        $patient = $facility->patients()->create([
            'mpi_id' => $mpi->id,
            'local_code' => $this->nextLocalCode($facility),
            'first_name' => $data['first_name'],
            'last_name' => $data['last_name'],
            'date_of_birth' => $data['date_of_birth'] ?? null,
            'gender' => $data['gender'] ?? null,
            'phone' => $data['phone'] ?? null,
            'address' => $data['address'] ?? null,
            'blood_group' => $data['blood_group'] ?? null,
            'allergies' => $data['allergies'] ?? null,
        ]);

        return response()->json(
            ['patient' => $this->payload($patient->load('mpi:id,hospiflow_code'))],
            201
        );
    }

    public function show(Request $request, int $patientId): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $patient = Patient::inFacility($facility)
            ->with('mpi:id,hospiflow_code')
            ->findOrFail($patientId);

        return response()->json(['patient' => $this->payload($patient)]);
    }

    public function update(UpdatePatientRequest $request, int $patientId): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);
        $patient = Patient::inFacility($facility)->findOrFail($patientId);
        $data = $request->validated();

        $updatable = ['first_name', 'last_name', 'date_of_birth', 'gender', 'phone', 'address', 'blood_group', 'allergies'];
        $payload = [];
        foreach ($updatable as $field) {
            if (array_key_exists($field, $data)) {
                $payload[$field] = $data[$field];
            }
        }
        $patient->update($payload);

        return response()->json([
            'patient' => $this->payload($patient->fresh()->load('mpi:id,hospiflow_code')),
        ]);
    }

    private function portableLike(string $column): string
    {
        return "LOWER({$column}) LIKE LOWER(?)";    // ✅ CORRECT
    }

    private function likePattern(string $needle): string
    {
        return '%'.str_replace(['%', '_'], ['\\%', '\\_'], $needle).'%';
    }

    private function nextLocalCode(Facility $facility): string
    {
        $count = Patient::withTrashed()->where('facility_id', $facility->id)->count() + 1;

        return 'P-'.strtoupper($facility->code).'-'
            .str_pad((string) $count, 4, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT);
    }

    private function payload(Patient $patient): array
    {
        return [
            'id' => $patient->id,
            'local_code' => $patient->local_code,
            'hospiflow_code' => $patient->mpi->hospiflow_code ?? null,
            'first_name' => $patient->first_name,
            'last_name' => $patient->last_name,
            'date_of_birth' => $patient->date_of_birth?->format('Y-m-d'),
            'gender' => $patient->gender,
            'phone' => $patient->phone,
            'address' => $patient->address,
            'blood_group' => $patient->blood_group,
            'allergies' => $patient->allergies,
            'facility_id' => $patient->facility_id,
        ];
    }
}


===== api/app/Http/Controllers/PharmacyController.php ===== (294 lignes, 9 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Http\Controllers\Concerns\ResolvesFacility;
use App\Http\Requests\Pharmacy\DispenseRequest;
use App\Models\Consultation;
use App\Models\Dispensation;
use App\Models\Drug;
use App\Models\DrugLot;
use App\Models\Patient;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class PharmacyController extends Controller
{
    use ResolvesFacility;

    public function drugs(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->facility($request);

        $drugs = Drug::orderBy('name')->get();

        return response()->json([
            'drugs' => $drugs->map(fn ($d) => [
                'id' => $d->id,
                'name' => $d->name,
                'form' => $d->form,
                'dosage' => $d->dosage,
                'unit' => $d->unit,
                'is_active' => $d->is_active,
            ]),
        ]);
    }

    public function storeDrug(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $this->facility($request);

        $data = $request->validate([
            'name' => ['required', 'string', 'max:200', 'unique:drugs,name'],
            'form' => ['required', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'dosage' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'unit' => ['required', 'string', 'max:50'],
        ], [
            'name.required' => 'Le nom du médicament est obligatoire.',
            'name.unique' => 'Ce médicament existe déjà au catalogue.',
            'unit.required' => 'L\'unité de dispensation est obligatoire.',
        ]);

        $drug = Drug::create([
            'name' => $data['name'],
            'form' => $data['form'],
            'dosage' => $data['dosage'] ?? null,
            'unit' => $data['unit'],
            'is_active' => true,
        ]);

        return response()->json(['drug' => [
            'id' => $drug->id,
            'name' => $drug->name,
            'form' => $drug->form,
            'dosage' => $drug->dosage,
            'unit' => $drug->unit,
            'is_active' => $drug->is_active,
        ]], 201);
    }

    public function stock(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $expiringDays = $request->query('expiring_days');

        $query = DrugLot::inFacility($facility)
            ->with('drug:id,name,form,dosage,unit')
            ->orderBy('expires_at');

        if ($expiringDays !== null) {
            $limit = now()->addDays((int) $expiringDays)->endOfDay();

            $query->whereNotNull('expires_at')
                ->where('expires_at', '<=', $limit)
                ->where('expires_at', '>', now())
                ->where('quantity', '>', 0);
        }

        return response()->json([
            'lots' => $query->get()
                ->map(fn ($lot) => $this->lotPayload($lot)),
        ]);
    }

    public function storeStock(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $data = $request->validate([
            'drug_id' => ['required', 'integer', 'exists:drugs,id'],
            'lot_number' => ['nullable', 'string', 'max:100'],
            'quantity' => ['required', 'integer', 'min:1', 'max:1000000'],
            'expires_at' => ['nullable', 'date', 'after:today'],
        ], [
            'quantity.required' => 'La quantité du lot est obligatoire.',
            'expires_at.after' => 'La date de péremption doit être future.',
            'drug_id.exists' => 'Médicament inconnu.',
        ]);

        $drug = Drug::where('is_active', true)->find($data['drug_id']);

        if (! $drug) {
            abort(422, 'Ce médicament est inconnu ou inactif.');
        }

        $lot = DrugLot::create([
            'facility_id' => $facility->id,
            'drug_id' => $drug->id,
            'lot_number' => $data['lot_number'] ?? null,
            'quantity' => $data['quantity'],
            'expires_at' => $data['expires_at'] ?? null,
        ]);

        return response()->json(
            ['lot' => $this->lotPayload($lot->load('drug:id,name,form,dosage,unit'))],
            201
        );
    }

    public function dispensations(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $query = Dispensation::inFacility($facility)
            ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name', 'drug:id,name,unit', 'dispenser:id,name'])
            ->orderByDesc('created_at');

        $patientId = $request->query('patient_id');

        if ($patientId !== null) {
            $query->where('patient_id', (int) $patientId);
        }

        return response()->json([
            'dispensations' => $query->get()
                ->map(fn ($d) => $this->dispensationPayload($d)),
        ]);
    }

    public function dispense(DispenseRequest $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);
        $data = $request->validated();

        $patient = Patient::inFacility($facility)->find($data['patient_id']);

        if (! $patient) {
            abort(422, 'Ce patient n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
        }

        $consultation = null;

        if (! empty($data['consultation_id'])) {
            $consultation = Consultation::inFacility($facility)->find($data['consultation_id']);

            if (! $consultation) {
                abort(422, 'Cette consultation n\'existe pas dans votre établissement.');
            }
        }

        $drug = Drug::where('is_active', true)->find($data['drug_id']);

        if (! $drug) {
            abort(422, 'Ce médicament est inconnu ou inactif.');
        }

        $quantity = $data['quantity'];
        $notes = $data['notes'] ?? null;

        [$dispensation, $remaining] = DB::transaction(function () use ($facility, $patient, $consultation, $drug, $quantity, $notes, $request) {
            $lots = DrugLot::inFacility($facility)
                ->where('drug_id', $drug->id)
                ->where('quantity', '>', 0)
                ->where(function ($q) {
                    $q->whereNull('expires_at')->orWhere('expires_at', '>', now());
                })
                ->orderBy('expires_at')
                ->lockForUpdate()
                ->get();

            $available = $lots->sum('quantity');

            if ($available < $quantity) {
                abort(422, 'Stock insuffisant : '.$available.' '.$drug->unit.'(s) disponible(s).');
            }

            $left = $quantity;

            foreach ($lots as $lot) {
                if ($left <= 0) {
                    break;
                }

                $take = min($lot->quantity, $left);
                $lot->quantity -= $take;
                $lot->save();
                $left -= $take;
            }

            $record = Dispensation::create([
                'facility_id' => $facility->id,
                'patient_id' => $patient->id,
                'consultation_id' => $consultation?->id,
                'drug_id' => $drug->id,
                'dispensed_by' => $request->user()->id,
                'quantity' => $quantity,
                'notes' => $notes,
            ]);

            return [$record, $available - $quantity];
        });

        return response()->json([
            'dispensation' => $this->dispensationPayload($dispensation->load(
                'patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name',
                'drug:id,name,unit',
                'dispenser:id,name'
            )),
            'remaining_stock' => $remaining,
        ], 201);
    }

    public function showDispensation(Request $request, int $dispensationId): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $dispensation = Dispensation::inFacility($facility)
            ->with(['patient:id,local_code,first_name,last_name', 'drug:id,name,unit', 'dispenser:id,name'])
            ->findOrFail($dispensationId);

        return response()->json([
            'dispensation' => $this->dispensationPayload($dispensation),
        ]);
    }

    private function lotPayload(DrugLot $lot): array
    {
        $expiringSoon = $lot->expires_at !== null
            && $lot->expires_at->isFuture()
            && $lot->expires_at->lessThanOrEqualTo(now()->addDays(30))
            && $lot->quantity > 0;

        return [
            'id' => $lot->id,
            'drug' => [
                'id' => $lot->drug->id,
                'name' => $lot->drug->name,
                'form' => $lot->drug->form,
                'dosage' => $lot->drug->dosage,
                'unit' => $lot->drug->unit,
            ],
            'lot_number' => $lot->lot_number,
            'quantity' => $lot->quantity,
            'expires_at' => $lot->expires_at?->format('Y-m-d'),
            'expired' => $lot->expires_at !== null && $lot->expires_at->isPast(),
            'expiring_soon' => $expiringSoon,
        ];
    }

    private function dispensationPayload(Dispensation $d): array
    {
        return [
            'id' => $d->id,
            'patient' => [
                'id' => $d->patient->id,
                'local_code' => $d->patient->local_code,
                'first_name' => $d->patient->first_name,
                'last_name' => $d->patient->last_name,
            ],
            'drug' => [
                'id' => $d->drug->id,
                'name' => $d->drug->name,
                'unit' => $d->drug->unit,
            ],
            'quantity' => $d->quantity,
            'dispensed_by' => $d->dispenser?->name,
            'consultation_id' => $d->consultation_id,
            'notes' => $d->notes,
            'created_at' => $d->created_at->format('Y-m-d H:i'),
        ];
    }
}


===== api/app/Http/Controllers/ReportController.php ===== (224 lignes, 8 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Http\Controllers;

use App\Http\Controllers\Concerns\ResolvesFacility;
use Illuminate\Http\JsonResponse;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Support\Carbon;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\DB;

class ReportController extends Controller
{
    use ResolvesFacility;

    private const LOW_STOCK_THRESHOLD = 20;

    private const EXPIRY_WINDOW_DAYS = 60;

    public function overview(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);
        [$from, $to] = $this->period($request);

        $fromAt = $from.' 00:00:00';
        $toAt = $to.' 23:59:59';

        $patientsTotal = DB::table('patients')
            ->where('facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->count();

        $newPatients = DB::table('patients')
            ->where('facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('created_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->count();

        $appointmentsByStatus = DB::table('appointments')
            ->where('facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('scheduled_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->groupBy('status')
            ->selectRaw('status, count(*) as total')
            ->get()
            ->mapWithKeys(fn ($row) => [$row->status => (int) $row->total]);

        $consultationsTotal = DB::table('consultations')
            ->where('facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('created_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->count();

        $labRequestsByStatus = DB::table('lab_requests')
            ->where('facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('created_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->groupBy('status')
            ->selectRaw('status, count(*) as total')
            ->get()
            ->mapWithKeys(fn ($row) => [$row->status => (int) $row->total]);

        $dispensationsTotal = DB::table('dispensations')
            ->where('facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('created_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->count();

        $invoices = DB::table('invoices')
            ->where('facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('created_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->where('status', '!=', 'cancelled')
            ->get(['id', 'total_amount']);

        $billedTotal = (int) $invoices->sum('total_amount');

        $paidInPeriod = (int) DB::table('payments')
            ->join('invoices', 'payments.invoice_id', '=', 'invoices.id')
            ->where('invoices.facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('payments.paid_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->sum('payments.amount');

        $issuedInvoices = DB::table('invoices')
            ->where('facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->where('status', 'issued')
            ->get(['id', 'total_amount']);

        $issuedBilled = (int) $issuedInvoices->sum('total_amount');

        $issuedPaid = (int) DB::table('payments')
            ->whereIn('invoice_id', $issuedInvoices->pluck('id'))
            ->sum('amount');

        $outstandingTotal = $issuedBilled - $issuedPaid;

        $recoveryRate = null;

        if ($billedTotal > 0) {
            $recoveryRate = round($paidInPeriod * 100 / $billedTotal, 1);
        }

        return response()->json([
            'report' => [
                'facility' => ['id' => $facility->id, 'code' => $facility->code],
                'period' => ['from' => $from, 'to' => $to],
                'patients' => [
                    'total' => $patientsTotal,
                    'new_in_period' => $newPatients,
                ],
                'appointments_by_status' => $appointmentsByStatus,
                'consultations_in_period' => $consultationsTotal,
                'lab_requests_by_status' => $labRequestsByStatus,
                'dispensations_in_period' => $dispensationsTotal,
                'billing' => [
                    'invoices_in_period' => $invoices->count(),
                    'billed_in_period' => $billedTotal,
                    'paid_in_period' => $paidInPeriod,
                    'outstanding_total' => $outstandingTotal,
                    'recovery_rate_percent' => $recoveryRate,
                ],
            ],
        ]);
    }

    public function revenue(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);
        [$from, $to] = $this->period($request);

        $fromAt = $from.' 00:00:00';
        $toAt = $to.' 23:59:59';

        // VAGUE1: revenue portable — regroupement jour calculé en PHP
        $rawPayments = DB::table('payments')
            ->join('invoices', 'payments.invoice_id', '=', 'invoices.id')
            ->where('invoices.facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('payments.paid_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->get(['payments.paid_at', 'payments.amount']);

        $daily = $rawPayments
            ->groupBy(fn ($row) => substr((string) $row->paid_at, 0, 10))
            ->map(fn ($rows, $day) => (object) ['day' => $day, 'total' => $rows->sum('amount')])
            ->sortKeys()
            ->values();

        $byMethod = DB::table('payments')
            ->join('invoices', 'payments.invoice_id', '=', 'invoices.id')
            ->where('invoices.facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->whereBetween('payments.paid_at', [$fromAt, $toAt])
            ->groupBy('payments.method')
            ->selectRaw('payments.method as method, sum(payments.amount) as total')
            ->get();

        return response()->json([
            'report' => [
                'period' => ['from' => $from, 'to' => $to],
                'paid_total' => (int) $daily->sum('total'),
                'daily' => $daily->map(fn ($row) => [
                    'day' => $row->day,
                    'total' => (int) $row->total,
                ]),
                'by_method' => $byMethod->map(fn ($row) => [
                    'method' => $row->method,
                    'total' => (int) $row->total,
                ]),
            ],
        ]);
    }

    public function stock(Request $request): JsonResponse
    {
        $facility = $this->facility($request);

        $lowStock = DB::table('drugs as d')
            ->join('drug_lots as s', 's.drug_id', '=', 'd.id')
            ->where('s.facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->groupBy('d.id', 'd.name')
            ->havingRaw('sum(s.quantity) <= ?', [self::LOW_STOCK_THRESHOLD])
            ->selectRaw('d.name as drug, sum(s.quantity) as total_quantity')
            ->get();

        // VAGUE1: stock portable — days_left calculé en PHP
        $expiringLots = DB::table('drug_lots as s')
            ->join('drugs as d', 'd.id', '=', 's.drug_id')
            ->where('s.facility_id', $facility->id)
            ->where('s.quantity', '>', 0)
            ->where('s.expires_at', '<=', now()->addDays(self::EXPIRY_WINDOW_DAYS))
            ->orderBy('s.expires_at')
            ->selectRaw('d.name as drug, s.quantity as quantity, s.expires_at as expiry_date')
            ->get()
            ->map(function ($row) {
                $expiry = Carbon::parse($row->expiry_date);
                $row->days_left = max(0, now()->startOfDay()->diffInDays($expiry->startOfDay(), false));

                return $row;
            });

        return response()->json([
            'report' => [
                'low_stock_threshold' => self::LOW_STOCK_THRESHOLD,
                'low_stock' => $lowStock->map(fn ($item) => [
                    'drug' => $item->drug,
                    'total_quantity' => (int) $item->total_quantity,
                ]),
                'expiring_window_days' => self::EXPIRY_WINDOW_DAYS,
                'expiring_lots' => $expiringLots->map(fn ($lot) => [
                    'drug' => $lot->drug,
                    'quantity' => (int) $lot->quantity,
                    'expiry_date' => $lot->expiry_date,
                    'days_left' => (int) $lot->days_left,
                ]),
            ],
        ]);
    }

    private function period(Request $request): array
    {
        $data = $request->validate([
            'from' => ['nullable', 'date'],
            'to' => ['nullable', 'date', 'after_or_equal:from'],
        ], [
            'to.after_or_equal' => 'La date de fin doit suivre la date de début.',
        ]);

        $from = $data['from'] ?? now()->subDays(29)->toDateString();
        $to = $data['to'] ?? now()->toDateString();

        return [$from, $to];
    }
}


===== api/app/Http/Middleware/EnsureFacilityIsActive.php ===== (60 lignes, 2 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use Closure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;

/**
 * Vérifie que l'utilisateur a un établissement actif avant chaque requête métier.
 * Remplace les duplications de currentFacility() dans les contrôleurs.
 *
 * Usage dans les routes :
 *   Route::middleware(['auth:sanctum', 'facility.active'])->group(...)
 */
class EnsureFacilityIsActive
{
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    {
        $user = $request->user();

        if (! $user) {
            return $this->jsonError(401, 'auth.unauthenticated', 'Non authentifié.');
        }

        $facility = $user->currentFacility;

        if (! $facility) {
            return $this->jsonError(
                409,
                'facility.none_active',
                'Aucun établissement actif. Choisissez-en un dans Établissements.'
            );
        }

        if (! $facility->is_active) {
            return $this->jsonError(
                403,
                'facility.inactive',
                'Votre établissement actif est désactivé.'
            );
        }

        // Expose la facility sur la request pour les contrôleurs
        $request->attributes->set('facility', $facility);

        return $next($request);
    }

    private function jsonError(int $status, string $code, string $message): Response
    {
        return response()->json([
            'error' => [
                'code' => $code,
                'message' => $message,
            ],
        ], $status);
    }
}


===== api/app/Http/Middleware/ResolveFacility.php ===== (28 lignes, 1 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Http\Middleware;

use Closure;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Symfony\Component\HttpFoundation\Response;

/**
 * Résout la facility courante depuis l'utilisateur authentifié et l'expose.
 * Placé APRÈS EnsureFacilityIsActive dans la pile (qui garantit sa présence).
 */
class ResolveFacility
{
    public function handle(Request $request, Closure $next): Response
    {
        if (! $request->attributes->has('facility')) {
            $facility = $request->user()?->currentFacility;

            if ($facility) {
                $request->attributes->set('facility', $facility);
            }
        }

        return $next($request);
    }
}

use Illuminate\Foundation\Http\FormRequest;

class PayInvoiceRequest extends FormRequest
{
    public function authorize(): bool
    {
        $invoice = Invoice::find($this->route('invoiceId'));

        return $invoice !== null
            && $this->user()->can('pay', $invoice);
    }

    public function rules(): array
    {
        return [
            'amount' => ['required', 'integer', 'min:1', 'max:100000000'],
            'method' => ['required', 'string', 'in:'.implode(',', Invoice::PAYMENT_METHODS)],
        ];
    }

    public function messages(): array
    {
        return [
            'amount.required' => 'Le montant encaissé est obligatoire.',
            'method.in' => 'Mode de paiement invalide.',
        ];
    }
}

===== api/app/Http/Requests/Pharmacy/DispenseRequest.php ===== (35 lignes, 1 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Http\Requests\Pharmacy;

    }

===== api/app/Models/MasterPatientIndex.php ===== (47 lignes, 1 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Model;
use Illuminate\Support\Str;

class MasterPatientIndex extends Model
{
    use HasFactory;

    public $incrementing = false;

    protected $keyType = 'string';

    protected $table = 'master_patient_index';

    protected $fillable = [
        'id',
        'hospiflow_code',
        'first_name',
        'last_name',
        'date_of_birth',
        'gender',
        'phone',
    ];

    protected static function booted(): void
    {
        static::creating(function (self $mpi) {
            if (empty($mpi->id)) {
                $mpi->id = Str::uuid()->toString();
            }

            if (empty($mpi->hospiflow_code)) {
                $mpi->hospiflow_code = 'HF-'.strtoupper(bin2hex(random_bytes(6)));
            }
        });
    }

    public function patients()
    {
        return $this->hasMany(Patient::class, 'mpi_id');
    }
}

===== api/app/Models/User.php ===== (58 lignes, 1 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Models;

use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Factories\HasFactory;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsTo;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\BelongsToMany;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Auth\User as Authenticatable;
use Illuminate\Notifications\Notifiable;
use Laravel\Sanctum\HasApiTokens;
use Spatie\Permission\Traits\HasRoles;

class User extends Authenticatable
{
    use HasApiTokens, HasFactory, HasRoles, Notifiable;

    protected $guard_name = 'web';

    protected $fillable = [
        'name',
        'email',
        'password',
        'phone',
        'is_active',
    ];

    protected $hidden = [
        'password',
        'remember_token',
    ];

    protected function casts(): array
    {
        return [
            'email_verified_at' => 'datetime',
            'password' => 'hashed',
            'is_active' => 'boolean',
        ];
    }

    public function facilities(): BelongsToMany
    {
        return $this->belongsToMany(Facility::class);
    }

    public function currentFacility(): BelongsTo
    {
        return $this->belongsTo(Facility::class, 'current_facility_id');
    }

    public function isMemberOf(Facility $facility): bool
    {
        return $this->facilities()
            ->where('facilities.id', $facility->id)
            ->exists();
    }
}


===== api/app/Policies/AppointmentPolicy.php ===== (38 lignes, 1 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Policies;

use App\Models\Appointment;
use App\Models\User;

class AppointmentPolicy
{
    public function viewAny(User $user): bool
    {
        return $user->can('patients.view');
    }

    public function view(User $user, Appointment $appointment): bool
    {
        return $user->can('patients.view')
            && $user->current_facility_id === $appointment->facility_id;
    }

    public function create(User $user): bool
    {
        return $user->can('appointments.manage');
    }

    public function update(User $user, Appointment $appointment): bool
    {
        return $user->can('appointments.manage')
            && $user->current_facility_id === $appointment->facility_id;
    }

    public function delete(User $user, Appointment $appointment): bool
    {
        return $user->can('appointments.manage')
            && $user->current_facility_id === $appointment->facility_id;
    }
}

namespace App\Policies;

use App\Models\DrugLot;
use App\Models\User;

class DrugLotPolicy
{
    public function viewAny(User $user): bool
    {
        return $user->can('pharmacy.view');
    }

    public function view(User $user, DrugLot $lot): bool
    {
        return $user->can('pharmacy.view')
            && $user->current_facility_id === $lot->facility_id;
    }

    public function create(User $user): bool
    {
        return $user->can('pharmacy.manage');
    }

    public function update(User $user, DrugLot $lot): bool
    {
        return $user->can('pharmacy.manage')
            && $user->current_facility_id === $lot->facility_id;
    }

    public function dispense(User $user, DrugLot $lot): bool
    {
        return $user->can('pharmacy.manage')
            && $user->current_facility_id === $lot->facility_id
            && $lot->quantity > 0;
    }
}


===== api/app/Policies/InvoicePolicy.php ===== (48 lignes, 1 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Policies;

use App\Models\Invoice;
use App\Models\User;

class InvoicePolicy
{
    public function viewAny(User $user): bool
    {
        return $user->can('billing.view');
    }

    public function view(User $user, Invoice $invoice): bool
    {
        return $user->can('billing.view')
            && $user->current_facility_id === $invoice->facility_id;
    }

    public function create(User $user): bool
    {
        return $user->can('billing.manage');
    }

    public function update(User $user, Invoice $invoice): bool
    {
        return $user->can('billing.manage')
            && $user->current_facility_id === $invoice->facility_id
            && in_array($invoice->status, ['draft', 'issued'], true);
    }

    public function pay(User $user, Invoice $invoice): bool
    {
        return $user->can('billing.manage')
            && $user->current_facility_id === $invoice->facility_id
            && $invoice->status === 'issued';
    }

    public function cancel(User $user, Invoice $invoice): bool
    {
        return $user->can('billing.manage')
            && $user->current_facility_id === $invoice->facility_id
            && in_array($invoice->status, ['draft', 'issued'], true)
            && $invoice->payments()->sum('amount') === 0;
    }
}


===== api/app/Policies/LabRequestPolicy.php ===== (40 lignes, 1 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Policies;

use App\Models\LabRequest;
use App\Models\User;

class LabRequestPolicy
{
    public function viewAny(User $user): bool
    {
        return $user->can('lab.view');
    }

    public function view(User $user, LabRequest $labRequest): bool
    {
        return $user->can('lab.view')
            && $user->current_facility_id === $labRequest->facility_id;
    }

    public function create(User $user): bool
    {
        return $user->can('lab.requests.manage');
    }

    public function update(User $user, LabRequest $labRequest): bool
    {
        return $user->can('lab.requests.manage')
            && $user->current_facility_id === $labRequest->facility_id
            && $labRequest->status !== 'completed';
    }

    public function storeResult(User $user, LabRequest $labRequest): bool
    {
        return $user->can('lab.results.manage')
            && $user->current_facility_id === $labRequest->facility_id
            && ! in_array($labRequest->status, ['completed', 'cancelled'], true);
    }
}


===== api/app/Policies/PatientPolicy.php ===== (44 lignes, 1 Ko)
<?php

namespace App\Policies;

use App\Models\Patient;
use App\Models\User;

class PatientPolicy
{
    public function viewAny(User $user): bool
    {
        return $user->can('patients.view');
    }

    public function view(User $user, Patient $patient): bool
    {
        return $user->can('patients.view')
            && $this->isSameFacility($user, $patient->facility_id);
    }

    public function create(User $user): bool
    {
        return $user->can('patients.manage');
    }

    public function update(User $user, Patient $patient): bool
    {
        return $user->can('patients.manage')
            && $this->isSameFacility($user, $patient->facility_id);
    }

    public function delete(User $user, Patient $patient): bool
    {
        return $user->can('patients.manage')
            && $this->isSameFacility($user, $patient->facility_id);
    }

    private function isSameFacility(User $user, ?int $facilityId): bool
    {
        return $facilityId !== null
            && $user->current_facility_id === $facilityId;
    }
}

===== api/bootstrap/app.php ===== (90 lignes, 3 Ko)
<?php

use App\Http\Middleware\EnsureFacilityIsActive;
use App\Http\Middleware\ResolveFacility;
use Illuminate\Auth\AuthenticationException;
use Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\ModelNotFoundException;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Application;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Configuration\Exceptions;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Configuration\Middleware;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;
use Illuminate\Validation\ValidationException;
use Laravel\Sanctum\Http\Middleware\EnsureFrontendRequestsAreStateful;
use Symfony\Component\HttpKernel\Exception\HttpExceptionInterface;

return Application::configure(basePath: dirname(__DIR__))
    ->withRouting(
        web: __DIR__.'/../routes/web.php',
        api: __DIR__.'/../routes/api.php',
        commands: __DIR__.'/../routes/console.php',
        health: '/up',
    )
    ->withMiddleware(function (Middleware $middleware): void {
        $middleware->alias([
            'facility.active' => EnsureFacilityIsActive::class,
            'facility.resolve' => ResolveFacility::class,
        ]);

        $middleware->api(prepend: [
            EnsureFrontendRequestsAreStateful::class,
        ]);
    })
    ->withExceptions(function (Exceptions $exceptions): void {
        $exceptions->shouldRenderJsonWhen(
            fn (Request $request) => $request->is('api/*') || $request->expectsJson(),
        );

        $exceptions->render(function (ValidationException $e, Request $request) {
            if (! $request->is('api/*') && ! $request->expectsJson()) {
                return null;
            }

            return response()->json([
                'error' => [
                    'code' => 'validation.failed',
                    'message' => 'Les données envoyées sont invalides.',
                    'fields' => $e->errors(),
                ],
            ], 422);
        });

        $exceptions->render(function (AuthenticationException $e, Request $request) {
            if (! $request->is('api/*') && ! $request->expectsJson()) {
                return null;
            }

            return response()->json([
                'error' => [
                    'code' => 'auth.unauthenticated',
                    'message' => 'Authentification requise.',
                ],
            ], 401);
        });

        $exceptions->render(function (ModelNotFoundException $e, Request $request) {
            if (! $request->is('api/*') && ! $request->expectsJson()) {
                return null;
            }

            return response()->json([
                'error' => [
                    'code' => 'resource.not_found',
                    'message' => 'Ressource introuvable.',
                ],
            ], 404);
        });

        $exceptions->render(function (HttpExceptionInterface $e, Request $request) {
            if (! $request->is('api/*') && ! $request->expectsJson()) {
                return null;
            }

            return response()->json([
                'error' => [
                    'code' => 'http.'.$e->getStatusCode(),
                    'message' => $e->getMessage() ?: 'Erreur serveur.',
                ],
            ], $e->getStatusCode());
        });
    })->create();

use App\Models\User;

return [

    /*
    |--------------------------------------------------------------------------

    | reset "broker" for your application. You may change these values
    | as required, but they're a perfect start for most applications.
    |
    */
