<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted } from 'vue'
import apiClient from '@/api/client'

const stats = ref({
  patients: 0,
  appointments: 0,
  consultations: 0,
  revenue: 0,
})

const upcomingAppointments = ref([])

onMounted(async () => {
  try {
    const { data: reportData } = await apiClient.get('/reports/overview')
    const r = reportData.report
    stats.value = {
      patients: r.patients.total,
      appointments: r.appointments_by_status.scheduled || 0,
      consultations: r.consultations_in_period,
      revenue: r.billing.paid_in_period.toLocaleString(),
    }

    const { data: apptData } = await apiClient.get('/appointments')
    upcomingAppointments.value = apptData.appointments.slice(0, 3)
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Erreur lors du chargement du dashboard', error)
  }
})

const formatTime = (dateString) => {
  const date = new Date(dateString)
  return date.toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
}
</script>


===== web/src/views/FacilitiesView.vue ===== (163 lignes, 5 Ko)
<script setup>
import { computed, onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();

const facilities = ref([]);
const currentFacilityId = ref(null);
const error = ref('');
const loading = ref(false);

const code = ref('');
const name = ref('');
const city = ref('');

const canManage = computed(() => auth.canManageFacilities);

async function load() {
  loading.value = true;
  error.value = '';
  try {
    const data = await api('/facilities', { token: auth.token });
    facilities.value = data.facilities;
    currentFacilityId.value = data.current_facility_id;
    auth.currentFacilityId = data.current_facility_id;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  } finally {
    loading.value = false;
  }
}

async function createFacility() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/facilities', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        code: code.value.trim(),
        name: name.value.trim(),
        city: city.value.trim() || null,
      },
    });
    code.value = '';
    name.value = '';
    city.value = '';
    await load();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function switchFacility(facilityId) {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await auth.switchFacility(facilityId);
    currentFacilityId.value = auth.currentFacilityId;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

onMounted(load);
</script>

<template>
  <main class="page">
    <header>
      <strong>HOSPIFLOW</strong>
      <router-link to="/">Tableau de bord</router-link>
    </header>

<section class="content">
      <h1>Établissements</h1>
      <p class="muted">
        Votre établissement actif détermine les données visibles dans les
        prochains modules.
      </p>

<p v-if="error" class="error">{{ error }}</p>

<table v-if="facilities.length">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Code</th>
            <th>Nom</th>
            <th>Ville</th>
            <th>Statut</th>
            <th></th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr v-for="facility in facilities" :key="facility.id">
            <td>{{ facility.code }}</td>
            <td>{{ facility.name }}</td>
            <td>{{ facility.city }}</td>
            <td>{{ facility.is_active ? 'Actif' : 'Désactivé' }}</td>
            <td>
              <button
                v-if="facility.id !== currentFacilityId"
                :disabled="!facility.is_active"
                @click="switchFacility(facility.id)"
              >
                Définir actif
              </button>
              <span v-else class="badge">Actif</span>
            </td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>

<p v-else-if="!loading">Aucun établissement rattaché à votre compte.</p>

<form v-if="canManage" class="create" @submit.prevent="createFacility">
        <h2>Créer un établissement</h2>
        <label>
          Code
          <input v-model="code" required maxlength="20" placeholder="CLIN01" />
        </label>
        <label>
          Nom
          <input v-model="name" required placeholder="Clinique Nouvelle Espérance" />
        </label>
        <label>
          Ville
          <input v-model="city" placeholder="Cotonou" />
        </label>
        <button type="submit">Créer</button>
      </form>
    </section>
  </main>
</template>

===== web/src/views/LabView.vue ===== (333 lignes, 10 Ko)
<script setup>
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();

const labRequests = ref([]);
const patients = ref([]);
const error = ref('');

const filterPatientId = ref('');
const filterStatus = ref('');

const patientId = ref('');
const panel = ref('');
const priority = ref('routine');
const notes = ref('');

const resultTargetId = ref(null);
const resultValues = ref('');
const resultConclusion = ref('');

const STATUS_LABELS = {
  pending: 'En attente',
  in_progress: 'En cours',
  completed: 'Complétée',
  cancelled: 'Annulée',
};

async function loadLabRequests() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();

if (filterPatientId.value) params.set('patient_id', filterPatientId.value);
    if (filterStatus.value) params.set('status', filterStatus.value);

const suffix = params.toString() ? '?' + params.toString() : '';
    const data = await api('/lab-requests' + suffix, { token: auth.token });
    labRequests.value = data.lab_requests;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function loadPatients() {
  try {
    const patientsData = await api('/patients', { token: auth.token });
    patients.value = patientsData.patients;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function createRequest() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/lab-requests', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        patient_id: Number(patientId.value),
        panel: panel.value.trim(),
        priority: priority.value,
        notes: notes.value.trim() || null,
      },
    });
    patientId.value = '';
    panel.value = '';
    priority.value = 'routine';
    notes.value = '';
    await loadLabRequests();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function setStatus(requestId, status) {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/lab-requests/' + requestId, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      token: auth.token,
      body: { status },
    });
    await loadLabRequests();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

function openResultForm(requestId) {
  resultTargetId.value = requestId;
  resultValues.value = '';
  resultConclusion.value = '';
}

async function submitResult() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/lab-requests/' + resultTargetId.value + '/result', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        values: resultValues.value.trim(),
        conclusion: resultConclusion.value.trim() || null,
      },
    });
    resultTargetId.value = null;
    await loadLabRequests();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

onMounted(async () => {
  await loadPatients();
  await loadLabRequests();
});
</script>

<template>
  <main class="page">
    <header>
      <strong>HOSPIFLOW</strong>
      <nav>
        <router-link to="/">Tableau de bord</router-link>
        <router-link to="/patients">Patients</router-link>
        <router-link to="/appointments">Rendez-vous</router-link>
        <router-link to="/consultations">Consultations</router-link>
        <router-link to="/facilities">Établissements</router-link>
      </nav>
    </header>

<section class="content">
      <h1>Laboratoire</h1>

<p v-if="error" class="error">{{ error }}</p>

<form class="filters" @submit.prevent="loadLabRequests">
        <label>
          Patient
          <select v-model="filterPatientId">
            <option value="">Tous</option>
            <option v-for="patient in patients" :key="patient.id" :value="patient.id">
              {{ patient.last_name }} {{ patient.first_name }} ({{ patient.local_code }})
            </option>
          </select>
        </label>
        <label>
          Statut
          <select v-model="filterStatus">
            <option value="">Tous</option>
            <option value="pending">En attente</option>
            <option value="in_progress">En cours</option>
            <option value="completed">Complétée</option>
            <option value="cancelled">Annulée</option>
          </select>
        </label>
        <button type="submit">Afficher</button>
      </form>

<table v-if="labRequests.length">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Date</th>
            <th>Patient</th>
            <th>Analyse</th>
            <th>Prescrit par</th>
            <th>Priorité</th>
            <th>Statut</th>
            <th>Résultat</th>
            <th>Actions</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr v-for="labRequest in labRequests" :key="labRequest.id">
            <td>{{ labRequest.created_at }}</td>
            <td>
              {{ labRequest.patient.last_name }} {{ labRequest.patient.first_name }}
              <br />
              <small>{{ labRequest.patient.local_code }}</small>
            </td>
            <td>{{ labRequest.panel }}</td>
            <td>{{ labRequest.requested_by }}</td>
            <td>
              <span class="badge" :class="labRequest.priority">
                {{ labRequest.priority === 'urgent' ? 'Urgent' : 'Routine' }}
              </span>
            </td>
            <td>
              <span class="badge" :class="labRequest.status">
                {{ STATUS_LABELS[labRequest.status] || labRequest.status }}
              </span>
            </td>
            <td>
              <template v-if="labRequest.result">
                {{ labRequest.result.conclusion || 'Saisi' }}
                <br />
                <small>{{ labRequest.result.resulted_at }} — {{ labRequest.result.resulted_by }}</small>
              </template>
              <template v-else>—</template>
            </td>
            <td class="actions">
              <button
                v-if="labRequest.status === 'pending'"
                @click="setStatus(labRequest.id, 'in_progress')"
              >
                Prendre en charge
              </button>
              <button
                v-if="labRequest.status === 'pending' || labRequest.status === 'in_progress'"
                @click="openResultForm(labRequest.id)"
              >
                Saisir résultat
              </button>
              <button
                v-if="labRequest.status === 'pending' || labRequest.status === 'in_progress'"
                @click="setStatus(labRequest.id, 'cancelled')"
              >
                Annuler
              </button>
            </td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>

<p v-else>Aucune demande d'analyse.</p>

<form
        v-if="resultTargetId !== null"
        class="create"
        @submit.prevent="submitResult"
      >
        <h2>Saisie du résultat (demande #{{ resultTargetId }})</h2>

<label>
          Valeurs
          <textarea
            v-model="resultValues"
            rows="4"
            required
            placeholder="Hb : 13,4 g/dL&#10;GB : 7 800/mm³&#10;CRP : 4 mg/L"
          ></textarea>
        </label>

<label>
          Conclusion
          <input v-model="resultConclusion" placeholder="Bilan normal" />
        </label>

<button type="submit">Valider le résultat</button>
        <button type="button" class="secondary" @click="resultTargetId = null">
          Annuler
        </button>
      </form>

<form class="create" @submit.prevent="createRequest">
        <h2>Nouvelle demande d'analyse</h2>

<label>
          Patient
          <select v-model="patientId" required>
            <option value="" disabled>— Choisir un patient —</option>
            <option v-for="patient in patients" :key="patient.id" :value="patient.id">
              {{ patient.last_name }} {{ patient.first_name }} ({{ patient.local_code }})
            </option>
          </select>
        </label>

<label>
          Type d'analyse
          <input v-model="panel" required placeholder="NFS, glycémie, CRP…" />
        </label>

<label>
          Priorité
          <select v-model="priority">
            <option value="routine">Routine</option>
            <option value="urgent">Urgent</option>
          </select>
        </label>

<label>
          Notes
          <input v-model="notes" placeholder="Contexte clinique éventuel" />
        </label>

<button type="submit">Prescrire</button>
      </form>
    </section>
  </main>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'

const email = ref('')
const password = ref('')
const loading = ref(false)
const error = ref('')
const router = useRouter()
const authStore = useAuthStore()

const handleLogin = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  error.value = ''
  try {
    await authStore.login(email.value, password.value)
    router.push('/app/dashboard')
  } catch (err) {
    error.value = err.response?.data?.error?.message || 'Identifiants incorrects.'
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}
</script>


===== web/src/views/PatientsView.vue ===== (212 lignes, 6 Ko)
<script setup>
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();

const patients = ref([]);
const query = ref('');
const error = ref('');
const loading = ref(false);

const firstName = ref('');
const lastName = ref('');
const dateOfBirth = ref('');
const gender = ref('');
const phone = ref('');
const address = ref('');
const bloodGroup = ref('');
const mpiCode = ref('');

async function load() {
  loading.value = true;
  error.value = '';
  try {
    const data = await api('/patients', { token: auth.token });
    patients.value = data.patients;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  } finally {
    loading.value = false;
  }
}

async function search() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    if (query.value.trim() === '') {
      await load();
      return;
    }
    const data = await api(
      '/patients/search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(query.value.trim()),
      { token: auth.token }
    );
    patients.value = data.results;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function createPatient() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/patients', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        first_name: firstName.value.trim(),
        last_name: lastName.value.trim(),
        date_of_birth: dateOfBirth.value || null,
        gender: gender.value || null,
        phone: phone.value.trim() || null,
        address: address.value.trim() || null,
        blood_group: bloodGroup.value.trim() || null,
        mpi_code: mpiCode.value.trim() || null,
      },
    });
    firstName.value = '';
    lastName.value = '';
    dateOfBirth.value = '';
    gender.value = '';
    phone.value = '';
    address.value = '';
    bloodGroup.value = '';
    mpiCode.value = '';
    await load();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

onMounted(load);
</script>

<template>
  <main class="page">
    <header>
      <strong>HOSPIFLOW</strong>
      <nav>
        <router-link to="/">Tableau de bord</router-link>
        <router-link to="/facilities">Établissements</router-link>
      </nav>
    </header>

<section class="content">
      <h1>Patients</h1>

<p v-if="error" class="error">{{ error }}</p>

<form class="search" @submit.prevent="search">
        <input
          v-model="query"
          placeholder="Nom, téléphone, code local ou code réseau HF-…"
        />
        <button type="submit">Rechercher</button>
      </form>

<table v-if="patients.length">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Code local</th>
            <th>Code réseau</th>
            <th>Nom</th>
            <th>Naissance</th>
            <th>Téléphone</th>
            <th>Groupe</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr v-for="patient in patients" :key="patient.id">
            <td>{{ patient.local_code }}</td>
            <td><code>{{ patient.hospiflow_code }}</code></td>
            <td>{{ patient.last_name }} {{ patient.first_name }}</td>
            <td>{{ patient.date_of_birth || '—' }}</td>
            <td>{{ patient.phone || '—' }}</td>

===== web/src/views/PharmacyView.vue ===== (363 lignes, 11 Ko)
<script setup>
import { computed, onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();

const drugs = ref([]);
const lots = ref([]);
const dispensations = ref([]);
const patients = ref([]);
const error = ref('');

const showOnlyExpiring = ref(false);

const drugId = ref('');
const drugName = ref('');
const drugForm = ref('comprimé');
const drugDosage = ref('');
const drugUnit = ref('');

const lotDrugId = ref('');
const lotNumber = ref('');
const lotQuantity = ref('');
const lotExpiresAt = ref('');

const dispensePatientId = ref('');
const dispenseDrugId = ref('');
const dispenseQuantity = ref('');
const dispenseNotes = ref('');

const lotsSorted = computed(() => {
  const sorted = [...lots.value].sort((a, b) => {
    const dateA = a.expires_at || '9999-12-31';
    const dateB = b.expires_at || '9999-12-31';
    return dateA.localeCompare(dateB);
  });
  return showOnlyExpiring.value
    ? sorted.filter((lot) => lot.expiring_soon)
    : sorted;
});

async function loadAll() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    const drugsData = await api('/pharmacy/drugs', { token: auth.token });
    drugs.value = drugsData.drugs;

const stockData = await api('/pharmacy/stock', { token: auth.token });
    lots.value = stockData.lots;

const dispensationsData = await api('/pharmacy/dispensations', {
      token: auth.token,
    });
    dispensations.value = dispensationsData.dispensations;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function loadPatients() {
  try {
    const patientsData = await api('/patients', { token: auth.token });
    patients.value = patientsData.patients;
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function createDrug() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/pharmacy/drugs', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        name: drugName.value.trim(),
        form: drugForm.value,
        dosage: drugDosage.value.trim() || null,
        unit: drugUnit.value.trim(),
      },
    });
    drugName.value = '';
    drugDosage.value = '';
    drugUnit.value = '';
    await loadAll();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function addLot() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/pharmacy/stock', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        drug_id: Number(lotDrugId.value),
        lot_number: lotNumber.value.trim() || null,
        quantity: Number(lotQuantity.value),
        expires_at: lotExpiresAt.value || null,
      },
    });
    lotDrugId.value = '';
    lotNumber.value = '';
    lotQuantity.value = '';
    lotExpiresAt.value = '';
    await loadAll();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

async function dispense() {
  error.value = '';
  try {
    await api('/pharmacy/dispensations', {
      method: 'POST',
      token: auth.token,
      body: {
        patient_id: Number(dispensePatientId.value),
        drug_id: Number(dispenseDrugId.value),
        quantity: Number(dispenseQuantity.value),
        notes: dispenseNotes.value.trim() || null,
      },
    });
    dispensePatientId.value = '';
    dispenseDrugId.value = '';
    dispenseQuantity.value = '';
    dispenseNotes.value = '';
    await loadAll();
  } catch (exception) {
    error.value = exception.message;
  }
}

onMounted(async () => {
  await loadPatients();
  await loadAll();
});
</script>

<template>
  <main class="page">

      </nav>
    </header>

            <th>Péremption</th>

                {{ drug.name }}
              </option>

          </label>

            N° de lot

          </label>

            Quantité

          </label>

            Péremption
            <input v-model="lotExpiresAt" type="date" />
          </label>

<button type="submit">Ajouter le lot</button>

          <h2>Dispenser au patient</h2>

<label>
            Patient
            <select v-model="dispensePatientId" required>

              <option v-for="patient in patients" :key="patient.id" :value="patient.id">
                {{ patient.last_name }} {{ patient.first_name }} ({{ patient.local_code }})
              </option>
            </select>

<label>
            Médicament

              <option value="" disabled>— Choisir —</option>
              <option v-for="drug in drugs.filter((d) => d.is_active)" :key="drug.id" :value="drug.id">
                {{ drug.name }}

          </label>

===== web/src/views/Reports.vue ===== (199 lignes, 6 Ko)
<script setup>
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue';
import { api } from '../api/client';
import { useAuthStore } from '../stores/auth';

const auth = useAuthStore();

function unwrap(res) {
  if (res && res.data) {
    return res.data;
  }
  return res;
}

function isoDate(offsetDays) {
  return new Date(Date.now() + offsetDays * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

const from = ref(isoDate(-29));
const to = ref(isoDate(0));
const loading = ref(false);
const error = ref('');
const overview = ref(null);
const revenue = ref(null);
const stock = ref(null);

const appointmentLabels = {
  scheduled: 'Planifiés',
  confirmed: 'Confirmés',
  done: 'Honorés',
  cancelled: 'Annulés',
  no_show: 'Absents',
};

const methodLabels = {
  cash: 'Espèces',
  mobile_money: 'Mobile Money',
  transfer: 'Virement',
};

function label(map, key) {
  return map[key] || key;
}

function xof(value) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('fr-FR').format(Number(value)) + ' XOF';
}

async function load() {
  loading.value = true;
  error.value = '';

try {
    const query = '?from=' + from.value + '&to=' + to.value;
    const o = unwrap(await api('/reports/overview' + query, { token: auth.token }));
    const r = unwrap(await api('/reports/revenue' + query, { token: auth.token }));
    const s = unwrap(await api('/reports/stock', { token: auth.token }));
    overview.value = o.report;
    revenue.value = r.report;
    stock.value = s.report;
  } catch (e) {
    error.value = 'Erreur de chargement : ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : String(e));
  }

loading.value = false;
}

onMounted(load);
</script>

<template>
  <section class="reports-page">
    <h1>Rapports &amp; statistiques</h1>

<p v-if="error" class="error">{{ error }}</p>

<form class="filters" @submit.prevent="load">
      <label>Du <input v-model="from" type="date" /></label>
      <label>au <input v-model="to" type="date" /></label>
      <button type="submit" :disabled="loading">
        {{ loading ? 'Chargement…' : 'Actualiser' }}
      </button>
    </form>

<div v-if="overview" class="grid">
      <article class="card">
        <h2>Patients</h2>
        <p class="big">{{ overview.patients.total }}</p>
        <p>{{ overview.patients.new_in_period }} nouveau(x) sur la période</p>
      </article>

<article class="card">
        <h2>Rendez-vous (période)</h2>
        <ul>
          <li v-for="(total, status) in overview.appointments_by_status" :key="status">
            {{ label(appointmentLabels, status) }} : {{ total }}
          </li>
        </ul>
      </article>

<article class="card">
        <h2>Activité clinique</h2>
        <p>Consultations : {{ overview.consultations_in_period }}</p>
        <p>Dispensations pharmacie : {{ overview.dispensations_in_period }}</p>
        <ul>
          <li v-for="(total, status) in overview.lab_requests_by_status" :key="status">
            Labo « {{ status }} » : {{ total }}
          </li>
        </ul>
      </article>

<article class="card">
        <h2>Facturation (période)</h2>
        <p>Factures émises : {{ overview.billing.invoices_in_period }}</p>
        <p>Facturé : {{ xof(overview.billing.billed_in_period) }}</p>
        <p>Encaissé : {{ xof(overview.billing.paid_in_period) }}</p>
        <p>Reste à recouvrer (factures émises) : {{ xof(overview.billing.outstanding_total) }}</p>
        <p>
          Recouvrement :
          {{ overview.billing.recovery_rate_percent === null ? '—' : overview.billing.recovery_rate_percent + ' %' }}
        </p>
      </article>
    </div>

<div v-if="revenue" class="grid">
      <article class="card">
        <h2>Encaissements par jour</h2>
        <p class="big">{{ xof(revenue.paid_total) }}</p>
        <table>
          <thead><tr><th>Jour</th><th>Montant</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            <tr v-for="day in revenue.daily" :key="day.day">
              <td>{{ day.day }}</td>
              <td>{{ xof(day.total) }}</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </article>

<article class="card">
        <h2>Par mode de paiement</h2>
        <table>
          <thead><tr><th>Mode</th><th>Montant</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            <tr v-for="entry in revenue.by_method" :key="entry.method">
              <td>{{ label(methodLabels, entry.method) }}</td>
              <td>{{ xof(entry.total) }}</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </article>
    </div>

<div v-if="stock" class="grid">
      <article class="card">
        <h2>Stocks bas (≤ {{ stock.low_stock_threshold }})</h2>
        <table>
          <thead><tr><th>Médicament</th><th>Quantité</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            <tr v-for="item in stock.low_stock" :key="item.drug">
              <td>{{ item.drug }}</td>
              <td>{{ item.total_quantity }}</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </article>

<article class="card">
        <h2>Péremption ≤ {{ stock.expiring_window_days }} jours</h2>
        <table>
          <thead><tr><th>Médicament</th><th>Qté</th><th>Échéance</th><th>Jours</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            <tr v-for="lot in stock.expiring_lots" :key="lot.drug + '-' + lot.expiry_date">
              <td>{{ lot.drug }}</td>
              <td>{{ lot.quantity }}</td>
              <td>{{ lot.expiry_date }}</td>
              <td>{{ lot.days_left }}</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </article>
    </div>
  </section>
</template>

===== context.md ===== (138 lignes, 6 Ko)
# HOSPIFLOW — Contexte & Vision Projet

> **Dernière mise à jour** : 2026-11-17
> **État du code** : Vague 1 & 2 terminées (MVP sécurisé, testé, CI verte sur GitHub)
> **Vision Produit** : HealthTech professionnelle, SaaS multi-établissements, Cloud-first.
> **Repo** : https://github.com/Maxlechampion/hospiflow

---

## 1. 🎯 Vision & Décisions Stratégiques (MVP)

Pour faire de HOSPIFLOW une solution HealthTech convoitée, nous appliquons les règles suivantes :
- **Logiciel** : 100% Open Source et gratuit (Laravel, Vue 3, PostgreSQL).
- **Hébergement** : Gratuit en développement (local/Docker), professionnel (payant) uniquement pour le pilote avec de vrais patients.
- **Architecture** : Cloud-first, sans serveur local obligatoire. Mode hors ligne individuel par appareil (PWA).
- **Identification Patient** : MPI central HOSPIFLOW (identité réseau unique) + conservation des identifiants locaux de chaque établissement.
- **Dossier Médical** : Vue longitudinale consolidée, mais les données sources restent hébergées et gérées par chaque établissement (traçabilité de la provenance).
- **HOSPIFLOW SHARE** : Partage médical limité et progressif pour le MVP.
- **Périmètre MVP** : Établissements, Utilisateurs, Rôles, Patients (MPI), Admissions, Dossier médical (DME), Consultations, Rendez-vous, Lits/Hospitalisations, Dashboard, HOSPIFLOW SHARE (v1), Synchronisation.

*(Modules reportés : Pharmacie, Laboratoire, Finances, RH, IA. Le code actuel les couvre déjà en partie, mais l'accent produit reste sur le cœur clinique).*

---

## 2. 🛠 Stack Technique

- **Backend** : Laravel 13, PHP 8.3+ (API REST)
- **Frontend** : Vue.js 3 + TypeScript (PWA, stockage hors ligne avec IndexedDB / Dexie.js)
- **Base de données** : PostgreSQL (prod) / SQLite (dev & tests)
- **Auth** : Laravel Sanctum
- **RBAC** : Spatie Laravel Permission
- **Audit** : Spatie Activity Log
- **Tests** : Pest PHP 4
- **Formatage** : Laravel Pint
- **Analyse statique** : Larastan niveau 5 (PHPStan 2.x)
- **CI/CD** : GitHub Actions
- **Environnement** : Docker (à configurer pour la prod)
- **Maquettes/Conception** : Penpot, OpenAPI/Swagger

---

## 3. 🔒 Sécurité & Conformité (Intégrées dès le MVP)

La sécurité fait partie de l'architecture initiale (réalisée dans les Vagues 1 & 2) :
- **Isolation stricte** : Chaque requête métier est scopée par `facility_id` (Multi-tenant via Middleware `EnsureFacilityIsActive` et Policies Laravel).
- **Authentification** : Tokens Sanctum, rate limiting (`throttle:5,1` sur `/login`), anti-énumération d'e-mails.
- **Autorisation** : 6 Policies Laravel + Spatie.
- **Journalisation** : Audit log automatique sur les 5 entités sensibles (Patient, Consultation, Invoice, DrugLot, Appointment) avec `tapActivity()` pour tracer la `facility_id`.
- **Données** : Format d'erreur JSON uniforme, respect de la portabilité SQL (SQLite/PostgreSQL).

---

## 4. 🏗 Architecture du Code & Modules

### Multi-tenant (Cœur du système)
1. Middleware `EnsureFacilityIsActive` : bloque si l'utilisateur n'a pas d'établissement actif.
2. Trait `ResolvesFacility` : injecte `$this->facility($request)` dans les contrôleurs.
3. Policies : vérifient que `$user->current_facility_id === $model->facility_id`.

### Modules métier implémentés (M00 → M10)
| Module | Contrôleur | Statut |
|---|---|---|
| M00 — Auth | `AuthController` | ✅ |
| M01 — Établissements | `FacilityController` | ✅ |
| M02 — Patients (MPI) | `PatientController` | ✅ |
| M03 — Rendez-vous | `AppointmentController` | ✅ |
| M04 — Consultations | `ConsultationController` | ✅ |
| M05 — Laboratoire | `LabRequestController` | ✅ |
| M06 — Pharmacie | `PharmacyController` | ✅ |
| M07 — Facturation | `InvoiceController` | ✅ |
| M08 — Rapports | `ReportController` | ✅ |
| M09 — Audit | `AuditLogController` | ✅ |

---

## 5. 📊 État d'avancement & Tests

| Vague | Statut | Contenu |
|---|---|---|
| **Vague 1** | ✅ | Correctifs critiques (SQL portable, bugs `array_filter`, sécurité email) |
| **Lot A** | ✅ | Middlewares, 7 Form Requests, Rate limiting, JSON uniformes |
| **Lot B** | ✅ | Audit log (Spatie Activity Log) + intégration multi-tenant |
| **Lot B-bis** | ✅ | 6 Policies Laravel (Patient, Appointment, Consultation, Invoice, LabRequest, DrugLot) |
| **Lot B-ter** | ✅ | Form Requests migrés vers les Policies |
| **Lot C1** | ✅ | 40 tests Pest / 106 assertions (Auth, Patient, RDV, Facture, Audit, Policies) |
| **Lot C3** | ✅ | CI GitHub Actions + Larastan niveau 5 (0 erreur) |
| **Vague 3** | ⏳ | **À faire : Fonctionnel métier (Prescriptions, Tarification, Catalogue labo)** |

---

## 6. 🎯 Prochaine étape : Vague 3 (Fonctionnel Métier)

Les priorités pour la suite du développement :
1. **Prescriptions structurées** : tables `prescriptions` + `prescription_items` liés à `Drug`.
2. **Tarification médicaments** : ajouter `unit_price` sur `Drug` et lier la dispensation à une facture (`InvoiceItem`).
3. **Catalogue d'analyses labo** : tables `lab_panels` + `lab_panel_items`.
4. **Admissions & Lits** : tables `admissions` (lié au patient et à la facility).

---

## 7. 📐 Conventions de Développement

- **Code** : anglais (variables, méthodes, colonnes).
- **Messages utilisateur** : français.
- **Validation** : Form Requests (pas de `$request->validate()` dans les contrôleurs).
- **Autorisation** : Policies Laravel (pas de `$user->can('permission')` direct).
- **Portabilité SQL** : compatible SQLite **et** PostgreSQL (pas de `ilike`, `to_char`, `extract` — utiliser `LOWER()` et calculer en PHP).
- **Erreurs** : format uniforme `{error: {code, message, fields?}}`.
- **Tests** : Pest, DB `:memory:`, `RefreshDatabase`.
- **Style** : `./vendor/bin/pint` avant chaque commit.

---

## 8. 🔗 Fichiers clés & Commandes

**Fichiers critiques :**
- `api/routes/api.php` (Routes)
- `api/bootstrap/app.php` (Middlewares + JSON handler)
- `api/app/Http/Controllers/Concerns/ResolvesFacility.php` (Trait facility)
- `api/app/Http/Middleware/EnsureFacilityIsActive.php`
- `api/tests/Pest.php` (Config tests)
- `api/phpstan.neon` (Config Larastan)
- `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (Tests + Lint)
- `.github/workflows/static-analysis.yml` (Larastan)

**Commandes utiles :**
```bash
# Tests
cd api && ./vendor/bin/pest --colors=never

# Formatage
cd api && ./vendor/bin/pint

# Analyse statique
cd api && ./vendor/bin/phpstan analyse --memory-limit=2G

# CI locale (tout d'un coup)
cd api && ./vendor/bin/pint --test && ./vendor/bin/phpstan analyse && ./vendor/bin/pest

===== create-workflows.mjs ===== (136 lignes, 4 Ko)
// create-workflows.mjs — Crée les 2 fichiers .github/workflows/
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));

const FILES = {};

FILES['.github/workflows/ci.yml'] = `name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main, master, develop]
  pull_request:
    branches: [main, master, develop]
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  tests:
    name: Tests (PHP \${{ matrix.php }})
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        php: ['8.3']
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: api
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - name: Setup PHP
        uses: shivammathur/setup-php@v2
        with:
          php-version: \${{ matrix.php }}
          extensions: mbstring, pdo, pdo_sqlite, sqlite3, openssl, curl, fileinfo, zip
          coverage: none
          tools: composer:v2
      - name: Cache Composer
        uses: actions/cache@v4
        with:
          path: ~/.composer/cache/files
          key: composer-\${{ matrix.php }}-\${{ hashFiles('api/composer.lock') }}
          restore-keys: composer-\${{ matrix.php }}-
      - name: Install dependencies
        run: composer install --prefer-dist --no-progress --no-interaction
      - name: Copy .env
        run: cp .env.example .env
      - name: Generate app key
        run: php artisan key:generate
      - name: Execute tests
        run: ./vendor/bin/pest --colors=never

  lint:
    name: Code Style (Pint)
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: api
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - name: Setup PHP
        uses: shivammathur/setup-php@v2
        with:
          php-version: '8.3'
          coverage: none
          tools: composer:v2
      - name: Cache Composer
        uses: actions/cache@v4
        with:
          path: ~/.composer/cache/files
          key: composer-lint-\${{ hashFiles('api/composer.lock') }}
          restore-keys: composer-lint-
      - name: Install dependencies
        run: composer install --prefer-dist --no-progress --no-interaction
      - name: Check code style
        run: ./vendor/bin/pint --test
`;

FILES['.github/workflows/static-analysis.yml'] = `name: Static Analysis

on:
  push:
    branches: [main, master, develop]
  pull_request:
    branches: [main, master, develop]
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  larastan:
    name: Larastan (level 5)
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: api
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - name: Setup PHP
        uses: shivammathur/setup-php@v2
        with:
          php-version: '8.3'
          extensions: mbstring, pdo, pdo_sqlite, sqlite3
          coverage: none
          tools: composer:v2
      - name: Cache Composer
        uses: actions/cache@v4
        with:
          path: ~/.composer/cache/files
          key: composer-larastan-\${{ hashFiles('api/composer.lock') }}
          restore-keys: composer-larastan-
      - name: Install dependencies
        run: composer install --prefer-dist --no-progress --no-interaction
      - name: Run Larastan
        run: ./vendor/bin/phpstan analyse --memory-limit=2G --no-progress
`;

console.log('▶ Création des workflows GitHub Actions\n');

let created = 0;
for (const [rel, content] of Object.entries(FILES)) {
  const abs = path.join(__dirname, rel);
  if (fs.existsSync(abs)) {
    console.log(`○ ${rel} — existe déjà`);
    continue;
  }
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(abs), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(abs, content, 'utf8');
  console.log(`✔ ${rel} — créé`);
  created++;
}

console.log(`\n${created} fichier(s) créé(s)`);
console.log('\nProchaine étape :');
console.log('  git add .github/workflows/');
console.log('  git commit -m "Add GitHub Actions workflows"');
console.log('  git push');

===== fix-frontend-api.mjs ===== (114 lignes, 4 Ko)
// fix-frontend-api.mjs — Corrige les imports API et le store
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const WEB = path.join(__dirname, 'web');

const C = { reset: '\x1b[0m', green: '\x1b[32m', red: '\x1b[31m', yellow: '\x1b[33m', cyan: '\x1b[36m', bold: '\x1b[1m' };
const log = (icon, msg, color = 'green') => console.log(`${C[color]}${icon}${C.reset} ${msg}`);

log('', `\n${C.bold}▶ Correction du Frontend (Imports & Store)${C.reset}\n`);

// =============================================================================
// 1. Corriger client.js (export default ET export nommé)
// =============================================================================
const clientPath = path.join(WEB, 'src', 'api', 'client.js');
fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(clientPath), { recursive: true });

const clientContent = `import axios from 'axios'

const apiClient = axios.create({
  baseURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:8000/api',
  headers: {
    'Accept': 'application/json',
    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
  },
})

apiClient.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('token')
  if (token) {
    config.headers.Authorization = \`Bearer \${token}\`
  }
  return config
})

apiClient.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => response,
  (error) => {
    if (error.response?.status === 401) {
      localStorage.removeItem('token')
      window.location.href = '/login'
    }
    return Promise.reject(error)
  }
)

// On exporte des deux façons pour éviter les erreurs d'import
export default apiClient
export const api = apiClient
`;

fs.writeFileSync(clientPath, clientContent, 'utf8');
log('✔', 'src/api/client.js — corrigé (export default + export nommé)');

// =============================================================================
// 2. Corriger le store (auth.js) pour utiliser le bon import
// =============================================================================
const authPath = path.join(WEB, 'src', 'stores', 'auth.js');
fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(authPath), { recursive: true });

const authContent = `import { defineStore } from 'pinia'
import apiClient from '@/api/client'

export const useAuthStore = defineStore('auth', {
  state: () => ({
    token: localStorage.getItem('token') || null,
    user: null,
  }),
  getters: {
    isAuthenticated: (state) => !!state.token,
  },
  actions: {
    async login(email, password) {
      const { data } = await apiClient.post('/auth/login', { email, password })
      this.token = data.token
      this.user = data.user
      localStorage.setItem('token', data.token)
    },
    logout() {
      this.token = null
      this.user = null
      localStorage.removeItem('token')
    },
    async fetchUser() {
      if (!this.token) return
      const { data } = await apiClient.get('/auth/me')
      this.user = data.user
    }
  }
})
`;

fs.writeFileSync(authPath, authContent, 'utf8');
log('✔', 'src/stores/auth.js — corrigé (utilise import par défaut)');

// =============================================================================
// 3. Vérifier le main.js (Import du CSS)
// =============================================================================
const mainPath = path.join(WEB, 'src', 'main.js');
if (fs.existsSync(mainPath)) {
  let main = fs.readFileSync(mainPath, 'utf8');
  if (!main.includes('./style.css')) {
    main = main.replace("import App from './App.vue'", "import App from './App.vue'\nimport './style.css'");
    fs.writeFileSync(mainPath, main, 'utf8');
    log('✔', 'src/main.js — import de style.css ajouté');
  }
}

log('', `\n${C.bold}${C.green}✅ Corrections appliquées.${C.reset}`);
log('', `\n${C.bold}Prochaine étape :${C.reset}`);
console.log(`  1. Redémarre Vite (Ctrl+C puis) : ${C.cyan}cd web && npm run dev${C.reset}`);
console.log(`  2. Ouvre ${C.cyan}http://localhost:5173${C.reset}`);

// fix-vite-config.mjs — Corrige vite.config.js avec l'alias @
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const WEB = path.join(__dirname, 'web');
const CONFIG = path.join(WEB, 'vite.config.js');

const NEW_CONFIG = `import { defineConfig } from 'vite'
import vue from '@vitejs/plugin-vue'
import path from 'node:path'
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url'

const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url))

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [vue()],
  resolve: {
    alias: {
      '@': path.resolve(__dirname, './src'),
    },
  },
  server: {
    port: 5173,
    proxy: {
      '/api': {
        target: 'http://localhost:8000',
        changeOrigin: true,
      },
    },
  },
})
`;

if (!fs.existsSync(WEB)) {
  console.error('❌ Dossier web/ introuvable');
  process.exit(1);
}

// Backup
if (fs.existsSync(CONFIG)) {
  const bak = CONFIG + '.bak';
  if (!fs.existsSync(bak)) fs.copyFileSync(CONFIG, bak);
  console.log('✔ Backup : vite.config.js.bak');
}

fs.writeFileSync(CONFIG, NEW_CONFIG, 'utf8');
console.log('✔ vite.config.js réécrit avec :');
console.log('  - alias @ → ./src');
console.log('  - proxy /api → http://localhost:8000');
console.log('\nProchaine étape :');
console.log('  cd web');
console.log('  (fermer le serveur npm run dev avec Ctrl+C)');
console.log('  npm run dev');

          <button @click="handleLogout" class="text-sm text-slate-500 hover:text-red-600 transition-colors font-medium">
            Déconnexion
          </button>
        </div>
      </header>

      <!-- Page Content -->
      <main class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
        <RouterView />
      </main>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'

const authStore = useAuthStore()
const router = useRouter()

const menuItems = [
  { name: 'dashboard', label: 'Tableau de bord', path: '/app/dashboard' },
  { name: 'patients', label: 'Patients', path: '/app/patients' },
  { name: 'appointments', label: 'Rendez-vous', path: '/app/appointments' },
  { name: 'consultations', label: 'Consultations', path: '/app/consultations' },
  { name: 'pharmacy', label: 'Pharmacie', path: '/app/pharmacy' },
  { name: 'lab', label: 'Laboratoire', path: '/app/lab' },
  { name: 'billing', label: 'Facturation', path: '/app/billing' },
  { name: 'reports', label: 'Rapports', path: '/app/reports' },
]

const handleLogout = () => {
  authStore.logout()
  router.push('/login')
}
</script>
`;

FILES['src/views/LoginView.vue'] = `<template>
  <div class="min-h-screen flex">
    <!-- Colonne gauche : Branding -->
    <div class="hidden lg:flex lg:w-1/2 bg-gradient-to-br from-primary-600 to-primary-900 p-12 flex-col justify-between relative overflow-hidden">

        <svg class="w-full h-full" viewBox="0 0 100 100" preserveAspectRatio="none">
          <path d="M0 100 L100 0 L100 100 Z" fill="white" />
        </svg>
      </div>

      <div class="relative z-10">
        <div class="flex items-center space-x-3">
          <div class="w-10 h-10 bg-white rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center">
            <span class="text-primary-600 font-bold text-xl">H</span>
          </div>
          <span class="text-white text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">HOSPIFLOW</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="relative z-10">
        <h1 class="text-4xl font-bold text-white leading-tight mb-4">
          La gestion hospitalière,<br>simplifiée et sécurisée.
        </h1>
        <p class="text-primary-100 text-lg">
          Connectez-vous pour accéder à votre espace de travail et gérer vos patients en toute sérénité.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div class="relative z-10 text-primary-200 text-sm">
        © 2026 HOSPIFLOW. Tous droits réservés.
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Colonne droite : Formulaire -->
    <div class="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-8 bg-surface">
      <div class="w-full max-w-md">
        <div class="text-center mb-10 lg:hidden">
          <span class="text-3xl font-bold text-primary-600">HOSPIFLOW</span>
        </div>

        <div class="mb-8">
          <h2 class="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 mb-2">Bienvenue</h2>
          <p class="text-slate-500">Veuillez vous connecter à votre compte.</p>
        </div>

        <form @submit.prevent="handleLogin" class="space-y-6">
          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-2">Adresse e-mail</label>
            <input
              v-model="email"
              type="email"
              required
              class="w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:border-primary-500 outline-none transition-all"
              placeholder="medecin@clinique.com"
            >
          </div>

          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 mb-2">Mot de passe</label>
            <input
              v-model="password"
              type="password"
              required
              class="w-full px-4 py-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary-500 focus:border-primary-500 outline-none transition-all"
              placeholder="••••••••"
            >
          </div>

          <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
            <label class="flex items-center">
              <input type="checkbox" class="rounded border-slate-300 text-primary-600 focus:ring-primary-500">
              <span class="ml-2 text-sm text-slate-600">Se souvenir de moi</span>
            </label>
            <a href="#" class="text-sm font-medium text-primary-600 hover:text-primary-700">Mot de passe oublié ?</a>
          </div>

          <div v-if="error" class="bg-red-50 text-red-600 p-3 rounded-lg text-sm border border-red-100">
            {{ error }}
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            :disabled="loading"
            class="w-full bg-primary-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-medium hover:bg-primary-700 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-primary-100 transition-all disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed flex justify-center items-center"
          >
            <span v-if="loading" class="animate-spin mr-2">⟳</span>
            {{ loading ? 'Connexion...' : 'Se connecter' }}
          </button>
        </form>

        <p class="mt-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
          Pas encore de compte ?
          <a href="#" class="font-medium text-primary-600 hover:text-primary-700">Contacter le support</a>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'

const email = ref('')
const password = ref('')
const loading = ref(false)
const error = ref('')
const router = useRouter()
const authStore = useAuthStore()

const handleLogin = async () => {
  loading.value = true
  error.value = ''
  try {
    await authStore.login(email.value, password.value)
    router.push('/app/dashboard')
  } catch (err) {
    error.value = err.response?.data?.error?.message || 'Identifiants incorrects.'
  } finally {
    loading.value = false
  }
}
</script>
`;

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// D. views/DashboardView.vue — Vue principale
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILES['src/views/DashboardView.vue'] = `<template>
  <div class="space-y-6">
    <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
      <div>
        <h1 class="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">Tableau de bord</h1>
        <p class="text-slate-500 text-sm mt-1">Bienvenue, voici un aperçu de votre établissement.</p>
      </div>
      <button class="bg-primary-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium hover:bg-primary-700 transition-colors shadow-sm">
        Nouvelle action
      </button>
    </div>

    <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
      <div class="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200">
        <p class="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1">Patients total</p>
        <p class="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">{{ stats.patients }}</p>
      </div>
      <div class="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200">
        <p class="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1">Rendez-vous (aujourd'hui)</p>
        <p class="text-3xl font-bold text-primary-600">{{ stats.appointments }}</p>
      </div>
      <div class="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200">
        <p class="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1">Consultations (période)</p>
        <p class="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">{{ stats.consultations }}</p>
      </div>
      <div class="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200">
        <p class="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 mb-1">Chiffre d'affaires</p>
        <p class="text-3xl font-bold text-emerald-600">{{ stats.revenue }} <span class="text-sm font-normal text-slate-500">XOF</span></p>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
      <div class="lg:col-span-2 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200 p-6">
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">Prochains rendez-vous</h2>
          <RouterLink to="/app/appointments" class="text-sm text-primary-600 hover:text-primary-700 font-medium">Voir tout</RouterLink>
        </div>
        <div class="space-y-4">
          <div v-for="appt in upcomingAppointments" :key="appt.id" class="flex items-center justify-between p-4 rounded-lg border border-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors">
            <div class="flex items-center space-x-4">
              <div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-primary-50 flex items-center justify-center text-primary-600 font-bold">
                {{ appt.patient.first_name[0] }}{{ appt.patient.last_name[0] }}
              </div>
              <div>
                <p class="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">{{ appt.patient.first_name }} {{ appt.patient.last_name }}</p>
                <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">{{ appt.reason }}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="text-right">
              <p class="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">{{ formatTime(appt.scheduled_at) }}</p>
              <span class="text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-blue-50 text-blue-700">Prévu</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div v-if="upcomingAppointments.length === 0" class="text-center py-8 text-slate-500 text-sm">
            Aucun rendez-vous à venir.
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200 p-6">
        <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 mb-4">Actions rapides</h2>
        <div class="space-y-3">
          <RouterLink to="/app/patients" class="flex items-center p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-100 hover:bg-primary-50 hover:border-primary-100 transition-colors group">
            <span class="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 group-hover:text-primary-700">+ Nouveau patient</span>
          </RouterLink>
          <RouterLink to="/app/appointments" class="flex items-center p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-100 hover:bg-primary-50 hover:border-primary-100 transition-colors group">
            <span class="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 group-hover:text-primary-700">+ Prendre un RDV</span>
          </RouterLink>
          <RouterLink to="/app/consultations" class="flex items-center p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-100 hover:bg-primary-50 hover:border-primary-100 transition-colors group">
            <span class="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 group-hover:text-primary-700">+ Nouvelle consultation</span>
          </RouterLink>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted } from 'vue'
import apiClient from '@/api/client'

const stats = ref({
  patients: 0,
  appointments: 0,
  consultations: 0,
  revenue: 0,
})

const upcomingAppointments = ref([])

onMounted(async () => {
  try {
    const { data: reportData } = await apiClient.get('/reports/overview')
    const r = reportData.report
    stats.value = {
      patients: r.patients.total,
      appointments: r.appointments_by_status.scheduled || 0,
      consultations: r.consultations_in_period,
      revenue: r.billing.paid_in_period.toLocaleString(),
    }

    const { data: apptData } = await apiClient.get('/appointments')
    upcomingAppointments.value = apptData.appointments.slice(0, 3)
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Erreur lors du chargement du dashboard', error)
  }
})

const formatTime = (dateString) => {
  const date = new Date(dateString)
  return date.toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
}
</script>
`;

// =============================================================================
// 3. Écriture
// =============================================================================

let written = 0;

for (const [rel, content] of Object.entries(FILES)) {
  const abs = path.join(WEB, rel);
  const bak = abs + '.bak';
  
  // Backup si le fichier existe déjà
  if (fs.existsSync(abs) && !fs.existsSync(bak)) {
    fs.copyFileSync(abs, bak);
    log('✔', `Backup : ${rel}.bak`, 'yellow');
  }

  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(abs), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(abs, content, 'utf8');
  log('✔', `${rel} — écrit`);
  written++;
}

// =============================================================================
// 4. Vérification du router
// =============================================================================
const routerPath = path.join(WEB, 'src', 'router', 'index.js');
if (fs.existsSync(routerPath)) {
  const routerContent = fs.readFileSync(routerPath, 'utf8');
  
  if (!routerContent.includes('DashboardLayout')) {
    log('⚠', 'Le router ne contient pas DashboardLayout. Mise à jour...', 'yellow');
    
    const newRouter = `import { createRouter, createWebHistory } from 'vue-router'
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/auth'

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(),
  routes: [
    { path: '/login', name: 'login', component: () => import('@/views/LoginView.vue') },
    {
      path: '/app',
      component: () => import('@/layouts/DashboardLayout.vue'),
      meta: { requiresAuth: true },
      children: [
        { path: '', redirect: '/app/dashboard' },
        { path: 'dashboard', name: 'dashboard', component: () => import('@/views/DashboardView.vue') },
        { path: 'patients', name: 'patients', component: () => import('@/views/PatientsView.vue') },
        { path: 'appointments', name: 'appointments', component: () => import('@/views/AppointmentsView.vue') },
        { path: 'consultations', name: 'consultations', component: () => import('@/views/ConsultationsView.vue') },
        { path: 'pharmacy', name: 'pharmacy', component: () => import('@/views/PharmacyView.vue') },
        { path: 'lab', name: 'lab', component: () => import('@/views/LabView.vue') },
        { path: 'billing', name: 'billing', component: () => import('@/views/BillingView.vue') },
        { path: 'reports', name: 'reports', component: () => import('@/views/Reports.vue') },
      ]
    },
    { path: '/', redirect: '/login' }
  ]
})

router.beforeEach(async (to) => {
  const authStore = useAuthStore()
  if (to.meta.requiresAuth && !authStore.isAuthenticated) {
    return { name: 'login' }
  }
})

export default router
`;
    
    const routerBak = routerPath + '.bak';
    if (!fs.existsSync(routerBak)) fs.copyFileSync(routerPath, routerBak);
    fs.writeFileSync(routerPath, newRouter, 'utf8');
    log('✔', 'src/router/index.js — mis à jour');
  } else {
    log('○', 'src/router/index.js — déjà conforme');
  }
}

// =============================================================================
// 5. Résumé
// =============================================================================
log('', `\n${C.bold}${C.green}✅ ${written} fichier(s) installé(s)${C.reset}`);
log('', `${C.bold}Prochaine étape :${C.reset}`);
console.log(`  ${C.cyan}cd web${C.reset}`);
console.log(`  ${C.cyan}npm run dev${C.reset}`);
console.log(`\n  Puis ouvre ${C.cyan}http://localhost:5173${C.reset}`);
